const { getDefaultConfig } = require('expo/metro-config');
const path = require('path');

const projectRoot = __dirname;
const monorepoRoot = path.resolve(projectRoot, '../..');

const config = getDefaultConfig(projectRoot);

// Watch all files in the monorepo
config.watchFolders = [monorepoRoot];

// Let Metro resolve packages from both the app and monorepo root node_modules
config.resolver.nodeModulesPaths = [
  path.resolve(projectRoot, 'node_modules'),
  path.resolve(monorepoRoot, 'node_modules'),
];

// Prevent pnpm symlink resolution loops
config.resolver.disableHierarchicalLookup = false;

module.exports = config;

FILE: apps\driver-app\App.tsx
SIZE: 2149 bytes
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet, useColorScheme } from 'react-native';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { useCleanTheme } from './src/theme/clean';
import { loadSession, clearSession, StoredSession } from './src/lib/auth/storage';
import { Boot } from './src/screens/v2/Boot';
import { Phone } from './src/screens/v2/Phone';
import { Otp } from './src/screens/v2/Otp';
import { Trips } from './src/screens/v2/Trips';

type Screen = 'boot' | 'phone' | 'otp' | 'trips';

export default function App() {
  const t = useCleanTheme();
  const scheme = useColorScheme();
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState<Screen>('boot');
  const [session, setSession] = useState<StoredSession | null>(null);
  const [pendingPhone, setPendingPhone] = useState('');

  console.log('[APP] render, screen =', screen);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const saved = await loadSession();
      if (saved) {
        console.log('[APP] restored session for', saved.user.phone);
        setSession(saved);
        setScreen('trips');
      } else {
        console.log('[APP] no saved session, starting at phone');
        setScreen('phone');
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: t.colors.bg }]}>
      <StatusBar style={scheme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light'} />
      {screen === 'boot' && <Boot />}
      {screen === 'phone' && (
        <Phone
          onSent={(phone) => {
            setPendingPhone(phone);
            setScreen('otp');
          }}
        />
      )}
      {screen === 'otp' && (
        <Otp
          phone={pendingPhone}
          onBack={() => setScreen('phone')}
          onVerified={(s) => {
            setSession(s);
            setScreen('trips');
          }}
        />
      )}
      {screen === 'trips' && session && (
        <Trips
          session={session}
          onLogout={async () => {
            await clearSession();
            setSession(null);
            setScreen('phone');
          }}
        />
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1 },
});

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../theme/tokens';

export function BusConBrand({
  size = 32,
  showSubtitle = true,
}: {
  size?: number;
  showSubtitle?: boolean;
}) {
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  return (
    <View style={styles.row}>
      <View
        style={[
          styles.mark,
          {
            width: size,
            height: size,
            borderRadius: size * 0.25,
            borderColor: t.colors.emerald,
            backgroundColor: t.colors.bg,
          },
        ]}
      >
        <Ionicons name="bus" size={size * 0.55} color={t.colors.emerald} />
      </View>
      <View>
        <Text
          style={[
            styles.title,
            {
              color: t.colors.textBright,
              fontSize: size * 0.5,
              fontFamily: t.fonts.mono,
            },
          ]}
        >
          BUS<Text style={{ color: t.colors.emerald }}>CON</Text>
        </Text>
        {showSubtitle && (
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.sub,
              {
                color: t.colors.textMuted,
                fontSize: Math.max(7.5, size * 0.24),
                fontFamily: t.fonts.mono,
              },
            ]}
          >
            CREW TERMINAL
          </Text>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  row:   { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 },
  mark:  { borderWidth: 1.5, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  title: { fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1 },
  sub:   { fontWeight: '700', letterSpacing: 0.8 },
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  Text,
  Modal,
  TouchableOpacity,
  TextInput,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { DriverTheme } from '../theme/tokens';
import { DelayReason } from '../types';

interface DelayModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmitDelay: (minutes: number, reason: DelayReason, notes?: string) => void;
}

const REASON_OPTIONS: Array<{ value: DelayReason; label: string }> = [
  { value: 'TRAFFIC', label: 'Heavy Traffic' },
  { value: 'WEATHER', label: 'Inclement Weather' },
  { value: 'BREAKDOWN', label: 'Vehicle Breakdown' },
  { value: 'ACCIDENT', label: 'Road Accident Ahead' },
  { value: 'ROAD_CLOSURE', label: 'Highway Road Closure' },
  { value: 'OTHER', label: 'Other Operational Delay' },
];

export function DelayModal({ visible, onClose, onSubmitDelay }: DelayModalProps) {
  const [selectedReason, setSelectedReason] = useState<DelayReason>('TRAFFIC');
  const [minutes, setMinutes] = useState('15');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    const minVal = parseInt(minutes, 10);
    if (isNaN(minVal) || minVal < 1 || minVal > 180) {
      Alert.alert('Validation Error', 'Delay must be between 1 and 180 minutes.');
      return;
    }
    onSubmitDelay(minVal, selectedReason, notes.trim());
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade">
      <View style={styles.backdrop}>
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.header}>
            <View>
              <Text style={styles.title}>Report Corridor Delay</Text>
              <Text style={styles.sub}>Broadcasts live delay minutes to passenger app</Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeBtn}>
              <Ionicons name="close" size={20} color={DriverTheme.colors.textBright} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.field}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>SELECT REASON CATEGORY</Text>
            <View style={styles.reasonsGrid}>
              {REASON_OPTIONS.map((item) => (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={item.value}
                  style={[styles.reasonPill, selectedReason === item.value && styles.reasonPillActive]}
                  onPress={() => setSelectedReason(item.value)}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.reasonText, selectedReason === item.value && styles.reasonTextActive]}>
                    {item.label}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              ))}
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.field}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>ESTIMATED DELAY (MINUTES: 1-180)</Text>
            <View style={styles.inputRow}>
              <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={18} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
              <TextInput style={styles.minInput} value={minutes} onChangeText={setMinutes} keyboardType="numeric" />
              <Text style={styles.minSuffix}>MINUTES</Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.field}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>ADDITIONAL DETAILS (OPTIONAL, MAX 200)</Text>
            <TextInput
              style={styles.notesInput}
              value={notes}
              onChangeText={setNotes}
              maxLength={200}
              placeholder="e.g. Heavy congestion near Nawabganj toll plaza"
              placeholderTextColor={DriverTheme.colors.textMuted}
              multiline
            />
          </View>

          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.submitBtn} onPress={handleSubmit}>
            <Ionicons name="radio" size={16} color="#020604" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
            <Text style={styles.submitBtnText}>BROADCAST DELAY TO PASSENGERS</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  backdrop: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.8)', justifyContent: 'center', padding: 20 },
  card: { backgroundColor: DriverTheme.colors.surface, borderRadius: 22, borderWidth: 1.5, borderColor: DriverTheme.colors.border, padding: 20, gap: 14 },
  header: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' },
  title: { color: DriverTheme.colors.textBright, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '900' },
  sub: { color: DriverTheme.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 10, marginTop: 1 },
  closeBtn: { padding: 4 },
  field: { gap: 6 },
  label: { color: DriverTheme.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 9, fontFamily: 'monospace', fontWeight: 'bold' },
  reasonsGrid: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 6 },
  reasonPill: { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 7, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: DriverTheme.colors.bg, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: DriverTheme.colors.border },
  reasonPillActive: { backgroundColor: DriverTheme.colors.emerald, borderColor: DriverTheme.colors.emerald },
  reasonText: { color: DriverTheme.colors.textSecondary, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '700' },
  reasonTextActive: { color: '#020604' },
  inputRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', backgroundColor: DriverTheme.colors.bg, borderRadius: 12, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: DriverTheme.colors.border, paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 10 },
  minInput: { flex: 1, color: DriverTheme.colors.emerald, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '900', fontFamily: 'monospace' },
  minSuffix: { color: DriverTheme.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 'bold', fontFamily: 'monospace' },
  notesInput: { backgroundColor: DriverTheme.colors.bg, borderRadius: 12, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: DriverTheme.colors.border, padding: 10, color: DriverTheme.colors.textBright, fontSize: 12, height: 60, textAlignVertical: 'top' },
  submitBtn: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', backgroundColor: DriverTheme.colors.emerald, paddingVertical: 14, borderRadius: 12, marginTop: 4 },
  submitBtnText: { color: '#020604', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '900', fontFamily: 'monospace' },
});

FILE: apps\driver-app\src\components\DocumentUploadModal.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  Text,
  Modal,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { DriverTheme } from '../theme/tokens';

interface DocumentUploadModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUploadSuccess: () => void;
}

export function DocumentUploadModal({ visible, onClose, onUploadSuccess }: DocumentUploadModalProps) {
  const [licenseUploaded, setLicenseUploaded] = useState(true);
  const [govtIdUploaded, setGovtIdUploaded] = useState(true);
  const [photoUploaded, setPhotoUploaded] = useState(false);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    if (!licenseUploaded || !govtIdUploaded || !photoUploaded) {
      Alert.alert('Missing Documents', 'Please upload all 3 documents: License, Government ID, and Profile Photo.');
      return;
    }
    setIsUploading(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setIsUploading(false);
      Alert.alert('Documents Submitted', 'Your documents have been submitted to the admin for KYC review.');
      onUploadSuccess();
      onClose();
    }, 1200);
  };

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="slide">
      <View style={styles.backdrop}>
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.header}>
            <View>
              <Text style={styles.title}>Driver KYC Documents</Text>
              <Text style={styles.sub}>PDF, JPG, or PNG up to 10MB per document</Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeBtn}>
              <Ionicons name="close" size={20} color={DriverTheme.colors.textBright} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.slots}>
            <View style={styles.slot}>
              <Ionicons name="card-outline" size={22} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
              <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 10 }}>
                <Text style={styles.slotTitle}>Commercial Driving License</Text>
                <Text style={styles.slotSub}>{licenseUploaded ? 'DL_UP78_2022.pdf (Attached)' : 'Required'}</Text>
              </View>
              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.slotBtn} onPress={() => setLicenseUploaded(true)}>
                <Text style={styles.slotBtnText}>{licenseUploaded ? 'REPLACE' : 'UPLOAD'}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.slot}>
              <Ionicons name="document-text-outline" size={22} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
              <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 10 }}>
                <Text style={styles.slotTitle}>Government Photo ID</Text>
                <Text style={styles.slotSub}>{govtIdUploaded ? 'Govt_ID.pdf (Attached)' : 'Required'}</Text>
              </View>
              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.slotBtn} onPress={() => setGovtIdUploaded(true)}>
                <Text style={styles.slotBtnText}>{govtIdUploaded ? 'REPLACE' : 'UPLOAD'}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.slot}>
              <Ionicons name="camera-outline" size={22} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
              <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 10 }}>
                <Text style={styles.slotTitle}>Driver Profile Photo</Text>
                <Text style={styles.slotSub}>{photoUploaded ? 'Photo.jpg (Attached)' : 'Required headshot'}</Text>
              </View>
              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.slotBtn} onPress={() => setPhotoUploaded(true)}>
                <Text style={styles.slotBtnText}>{photoUploaded ? 'REPLACE' : 'UPLOAD'}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>

          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.submitBtn} onPress={handleSubmit} disabled={isUploading}>
            <Text style={styles.submitBtnText}>
              {isUploading ? 'UPLOADING...' : 'SUBMIT DOCUMENTS'}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  Text,
  Modal,
  TouchableOpacity,
  TextInput,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { DriverTheme } from '../theme/tokens';
import { DriverProfile } from '../types';

interface ProfileDrawerProps {
  visible: boolean;
  driver: DriverProfile;
  onClose: () => void;
  onLogout: () => void;
  onOpenDocUpload: () => void;
  onUpdateName: (newName: string) => void;
}

export function ProfileDrawer({
  visible,
  driver,
  onClose,
  onLogout,
  onOpenDocUpload,
  onUpdateName,
}: ProfileDrawerProps) {
  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState(driver.name);

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      Alert.alert('Validation Error', 'Name cannot be blank.');
      return;
    }
    onUpdateName(name.trim());
    setIsEditing(false);
  };

  const getKycBadge = () => {
    if (driver.kycStatus === 'VERIFIED') {
      return { bg: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15)', border: DriverTheme.colors.emerald, text: DriverTheme.colors.emerald };
    }
    return { bg: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15)', border: DriverTheme.colors.warning, text: DriverTheme.colors.warning };
  };

  const kyc = getKycBadge();

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="slide">
      <View style={styles.backdrop}>
        <View style={styles.drawer}>
          <View style={styles.header}>
            <View style={styles.avatar}>
              <Text style={styles.avatarText}>{driver.name.charAt(0)}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 10 }}>
              <Text style={styles.title}>Driver Profile</Text>
              <Text style={styles.operatorText}>{driver.operatorName}</Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeBtn}>
              <Ionicons name="close" size={20} color={DriverTheme.colors.textBright} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <View style={[styles.kycCard, { backgroundColor: kyc.bg, borderColor: kyc.border }]}>
            <Ionicons name="shield-checkmark" size={16} color={kyc.text} />
            <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 8 }}>
              <Text style={[styles.kycStatusText, { color: kyc.text }]}>KYC STATUS: {driver.kycStatus}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.kycSub}>Admin compliance verification</Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.fields}>
            <View style={styles.fieldBox}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>LEGAL NAME</Text>
              {isEditing ? (
                <View style={styles.editRow}>
                  <TextInput style={styles.textInput} value={name} onChangeText={setName} />
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.saveBtn} onPress={handleSave}>
                    <Ionicons name="checkmark" size={16} color="#020604" />
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              ) : (
                <View style={styles.displayRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.fieldValue}>{driver.name}</Text>
                  <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setIsEditing(true)}>
                    <Ionicons name="pencil" size={14} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              )}
            </View>

            <View style={styles.fieldBox}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>PHONE NUMBER (IMMUTABLE)</Text>
              <View style={styles.immutableRow}>
                <Text style={styles.immutableText}>{driver.phone}</Text>
                <Ionicons name="lock-closed" size={14} color={DriverTheme.colors.textMuted} />
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.fieldBox}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>COMMERCIAL LICENSE</Text>
              <View style={styles.immutableRow}>
                <Text style={styles.immutableText}>{driver.licenseNumber} ({driver.experienceYears}y exp)</Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.actionRow} onPress={onOpenDocUpload}>
              <Ionicons name="cloud-upload-outline" size={18} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
              <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 10 }}>
                <Text style={styles.actionTitle}>Upload KYC Documents</Text>
                <Text style={styles.actionSub}>Driving license, government ID & photo</Text>
              </View>
              <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={16} color={DriverTheme.colors.textMuted} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.footer}>
            <Text style={styles.versionText}>Operator App v1.0.0 â€¢ Verified Field Tool</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.logoutBtn} onPress={onLogout}>
              <Ionicons name="log-out-outline" size={18} color="#FFFFFF" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
              <Text style={styles.logoutBtnText}>LOG OUT OF TERMINAL</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { View, Text, Animated, StyleSheet, Easing, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../theme/tokens';

const USE_NATIVE = Platform.OS !== 'web';

export function TransitLoader({
  size = 48,
  label = 'CONNECTING TRANSIT',
  loop = true,
}: {
  size?: number;
  label?: string;
  loop?: boolean;
}) {
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const bounce = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const dot1 = useRef(new Animated.Value(0.2)).current;
  const dot2 = useRef(new Animated.Value(0.2)).current;
  const dot3 = useRef(new Animated.Value(0.2)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loop) return;
    const bounceLoop = Animated.loop(
      Animated.sequence([
        Animated.timing(bounce, { toValue: -3, duration: 200, easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.ease), useNativeDriver: USE_NATIVE }),
        Animated.timing(bounce, { toValue:  1, duration: 200, easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.ease), useNativeDriver: USE_NATIVE }),
        Animated.timing(bounce, { toValue:  0, duration: 200, easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.ease), useNativeDriver: USE_NATIVE }),
      ]),
    );
    const dotLoop = (v: Animated.Value, delay: number) =>
      Animated.loop(
        Animated.sequence([
          Animated.delay(delay),
          Animated.timing(v, { toValue: 1,   duration: 300, useNativeDriver: USE_NATIVE }),
          Animated.timing(v, { toValue: 0.2, duration: 300, useNativeDriver: USE_NATIVE }),
          Animated.delay(400),
        ]),
      );
    bounceLoop.start();
    const l1 = dotLoop(dot1, 0);   l1.start();
    const l2 = dotLoop(dot2, 200); l2.start();
    const l3 = dotLoop(dot3, 400); l3.start();
    return () => { bounceLoop.stop(); l1.stop(); l2.stop(); l3.stop(); };
  }, [loop, bounce, dot1, dot2, dot3]);

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Animated.View style={{ transform: [{ translateY: bounce }] }}>
        <View
          style={[
            styles.busBox,
            {
              width: size,
              height: size,
              borderRadius: size * 0.22,
              borderColor: t.colors.emerald,
              backgroundColor: t.colors.surface,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Ionicons name="bus" size={size * 0.55} color={t.colors.emerald} />
        </View>
      </Animated.View>
      <View style={styles.labelRow}>
        <Text
          style={[
            styles.label,
            { color: t.colors.textMuted, fontFamily: t.fonts.mono, fontSize: Math.max(9, size * 0.22) },
          ]}
        >
          {label}
        </Text>
        <Animated.Text style={[styles.dot, { color: t.colors.emerald, opacity: dot1 }]}>.</Animated.Text>
        <Animated.Text style={[styles.dot, { color: t.colors.emerald, opacity: dot2 }]}>.</Animated.Text>
        <Animated.Text style={[styles.dot, { color: t.colors.emerald, opacity: dot3 }]}>.</Animated.Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root:     { alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 },
  busBox:   { borderWidth: 1.5, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  labelRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'flex-end' },
  label:    { fontWeight: '700', letterSpacing: 2 },
  dot:      { fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '900', lineHeight: 18, marginLeft: 1 },
});

import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, Text, ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../../theme/tokens';

type Variant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'danger';

export function Button({
  label,
  onPress,
  variant = 'primary',
  loading = false,
  disabled = false,
  style,
}: {
  label: string;
  onPress: () => void;
  variant?: Variant;
  loading?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  style?: ViewStyle;
}) {
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const bg =
    variant === 'primary'   ? t.colors.emerald :
    variant === 'danger'    ? t.colors.danger  :
    'transparent';
  const fg = variant === 'secondary' ? t.colors.emerald : '#052E16';
  const border = variant === 'secondary' ? t.colors.emerald : 'transparent';

  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={onPress}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      style={({ pressed }) => [
        styles.base,
        { backgroundColor: bg, borderColor: border, borderWidth: variant === 'secondary' ? 1 : 0, opacity: (disabled || loading) ? 0.55 : pressed ? 0.9 : 1 },
        style,
      ]}
    >
      {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color={fg} /> : <Text style={[styles.label, { color: fg }]}>{label}</Text>}
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  base:  { paddingVertical: 14, paddingHorizontal: 16, borderRadius: 10, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  label: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: '700' },
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import type { AuthStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/AuthStack';
import { Screen } from '../../../components/ui/Screen';
import { useAuth } from '../../../lib/auth/AuthContext';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../../../theme/tokens';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<AuthStackParamList, 'Otp'>;

export function OtpScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
  const { phone } = route.params;
  const { verifyOtp } = useAuth();
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const submit = async () => {
    setError(null);
    if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(otp)) { setError('Enter the 6-digit code'); return; }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await verifyOtp(phone, otp);
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e?.message ?? 'Invalid code');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Screen>
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>Enter the code</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.subtitle, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>Sent to {phone}</Text>

        <TextInput
          style={[styles.input, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, color: t.colors.textBright }]}
          value={otp}
          onChangeText={setOtp}
          keyboardType="number-pad"
          maxLength={6}
          placeholder="123456"
          placeholderTextColor={t.colors.textMuted}
          editable={!loading}
        />

        {error ? <Text style={[styles.error, { color: t.colors.danger }]}>{error}</Text> : null}

        <Pressable
          onPress={submit}
          disabled={loading}
          style={({ pressed }) => [
            styles.button,
            { backgroundColor: t.colors.emerald, opacity: loading ? 0.6 : pressed ? 0.9 : 1 },
          ]}
        >
          {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#052E16" /> : <Text style={styles.buttonText}>Verify</Text>}
        </Pressable>

        <Pressable onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
          <Text style={[styles.link, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>â† Change phone</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    </Screen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container:  { flex: 1, padding: 24, justifyContent: 'center' },
  title:      { fontSize: 22, fontWeight: '800', textAlign: 'center' },
  subtitle:   { fontSize: 14, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 6, marginBottom: 24 },
  input:      { borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 10, paddingHorizontal: 14, paddingVertical: 14, fontSize: 22, textAlign: 'center', letterSpacing: 10 },
  error:      { marginTop: 8, fontSize: 13, textAlign: 'center' },

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator } from 'react-native';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import type { AuthStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/AuthStack';
import { Screen } from '../../../components/ui/Screen';
import { useAuth } from '../../../lib/auth/AuthContext';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../../../theme/tokens';
import { BusConBrand } from '../../../components/BusConBrand';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<AuthStackParamList, 'Phone'>;

export function PhoneScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
  const { requestOtp } = useAuth();
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('+919876540011');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const submit = async () => {
    setError(null);
    if (!/^\+91\d{10}$/.test(phone)) { setError('Enter a valid +91 phone number'); return; }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await requestOtp(phone);
      navigation.navigate('Otp', { phone });
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e?.message ?? 'Could not send OTP');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Screen>
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.brand}>
          <BusConBrand size={44} />
        </View>

        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>Sign in to start driving</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.subtitle, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
          Use the phone number your operator invited you with
        </Text>

        <Text style={[styles.label, { color: t.colors.textSecondary }]}>Phone number</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={[styles.input, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, color: t.colors.textBright }]}
          value={phone}
          onChangeText={setPhone}
          keyboardType="phone-pad"
          autoCapitalize="none"
          placeholder="+919876543210"
          placeholderTextColor={t.colors.textMuted}
          editable={!loading}
        />

        {error ? <Text style={[styles.error, { color: t.colors.danger }]}>{error}</Text> : null}

        <Pressable
          onPress={submit}
          disabled={loading}
          style={({ pressed }) => [
            styles.button,
            { backgroundColor: t.colors.emerald, opacity: loading ? 0.6 : pressed ? 0.9 : 1 },
          ]}
        >
          {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#052E16" /> : <Text style={styles.buttonText}>Continue</Text>}
        </Pressable>

        <Text style={[styles.hint, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
          {process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_MOCK_OTP !== 'false'
            ? 'Dev mode â€” OTP is 123456'
            : 'Your operator invited you. Contact them if you cannot sign in.'}
        </Text>
      </View>
    </Screen>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, ScrollView, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, Pressable, RefreshControl } from 'react-native';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import { Screen } from '../../../components/ui/Screen';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../../../theme/tokens';
import { fetchManifest } from '../../../lib/api/scan.api';
import type { MainStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/MainStack';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<MainStackParamList, 'Manifest'>;

export function ManifestScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
  const { tripId } = route.params;
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const [activeStand, setActiveStand] = useState<string>('all');

  const q = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['manifest', tripId],
    queryFn: () => fetchManifest(tripId),
    refetchInterval: 30_000,
  });

  if (q.isLoading) {
    return <Screen><View style={styles.center}><ActivityIndicator size="large" color={t.colors.emerald} /></View></Screen>;
  }

  if (q.isError || !q.data) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <View style={styles.center}>
          <Text style={{ color: t.colors.danger, marginBottom: 12 }}>Could not load manifest</Text>
          <Pressable onPress={() => q.refetch()} style={[styles.retryBtn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.emerald }]}>
            <Text style={{ color: '#052E16', fontWeight: '700' }}>Retry</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </Screen>
    );
  }

  const m = q.data;
  const stands = m.byStand;
  const shown = activeStand === 'all' ? stands : stands.filter((s) => s.standId === activeStand);

  return (
    <Screen>
      <View style={[styles.header, { borderBottomColor: t.colors.border }]}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}>
          <Text style={[styles.back, { color: t.colors.emerald }]}>â† Trip</Text>
        </Pressable>
        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>
          {m.totalBoarded} of {m.totalPassengers} boarded
        </Text>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        horizontal
        showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
        contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 12, gap: 8 }}
        style={{ maxHeight: 56 }}
      >
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => setActiveStand('all')}
          style={[styles.chip, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: activeStand === 'all' ? t.colors.emeraldSoft : 'transparent' }]}
        >
          <Text style={{ color: activeStand === 'all' ? t.colors.emerald : t.colors.textMuted, fontWeight: '600', fontSize: 12 }}>
            All
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
        {stands.map((s) => (
          <Pressable
            key={s.standId}
            onPress={() => setActiveStand(s.standId)}
            style={[styles.chip, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: activeStand === s.standId ? t.colors.emeraldSoft : 'transparent' }]}
          >
            <Text style={{ color: activeStand === s.standId ? t.colors.emerald : t.colors.textMuted, fontWeight: '600', fontSize: 12 }}>
              {s.standName}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, gap: 16 }}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={q.isFetching} onRefresh={() => q.refetch()} tintColor={t.colors.emerald} />}
      >
        {shown.length === 0 ? (
          <Text style={{ color: t.colors.textMuted, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 24 }}>
            No passengers at this stand
          </Text>
        ) : shown.map((group) => (
          <View key={group.standId}>
            <Text style={[styles.standTitle, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>
              {group.standName} Â· {group.passengers.length} booking(s)
            </Text>
            {group.passengers.map((p) => (
              <View
                key={p.bookingId}
                style={[styles.card, { backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, borderColor: t.colors.border }]}
              >
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                  <Text style={[styles.name, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>
                    {p.passengerNames.join(', ')}
                  </Text>
                  <View style={[styles.badge, { backgroundColor: p.boardingStatus === 'BOARDED' ? t.colors.emeraldSoft : t.colors.surfaceElevated }]}>
                    <Text style={{ color: p.boardingStatus === 'BOARDED' ? t.colors.emerald : t.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '700' }}>
                      {p.boardingStatus}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
                <Text style={{ color: t.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, marginTop: 4 }}>
                  Seats {p.seatLabels.join(', ')} Â· {p.bookingReference}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
    </Screen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

  back:      { fontWeight: '600', fontSize: 14 },

FILE: apps\driver-app\src\features\scan\screens\ScanScreen.tsx

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, Platform, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import { Screen } from '../../../components/ui/Screen';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../../../theme/tokens';
import { verifyQr } from '../../../lib/qr/verify';
import { QrVerifyError } from '../../../lib/qr/types';
import { hasSeenNonce, recordNonce } from '../../../lib/qr/nonceCache';
import { postScan, fetchManifest } from '../../../lib/api/scan.api';
import { fetchDriverConfig } from '../../../lib/api/auth.api';
import { enqueue, listPending, markSynced, markFailed, countPending } from '../../../lib/scan/queue';
import type { MainStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/MainStack';
import type { QRPayloadV2 } from '../../../lib/qr/types';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<MainStackParamList, 'Scan'>;

type Result =
  | { kind: 'idle' }
  | { kind: 'verifying' }
  | { kind: 'valid'; payload: QRPayloadV2; serverResult: 'VALID' | 'DUPLICATE'; msg?: string }
  | { kind: 'queued'; payload: QRPayloadV2 }
  | { kind: 'invalid'; code: string; message: string };

const isWeb = Platform.OS === 'web';

export function ScanScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
  const { tripId, scheduleId } = route.params;
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  const [wire, setWire] = useState('');
  const [manualRef, setManualRef] = useState('');
  const [manualName, setManualName] = useState('');
  const [result, setResult] = useState<Result>({ kind: 'idle' });
  const [pendingCount, setPendingCount] = useState({ pending: 0, failed: 0 });

  const cfgQ = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['driver-config'],
    queryFn: fetchDriverConfig,
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });

  const refreshCounts = useCallback(async () => {
    try { setPendingCount(await countPending(tripId)); } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }, [tripId]);

  useEffect(() => { void refreshCounts(); }, [refreshCounts]);

  const submitToServer = useCallback(
    async (qrPayload: string, payload: QRPayloadV2) => {
      const scannedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      try {
        const res = await postScan(tripId, { qrPayload, scannedAt });
        recordNonce(payload.nonce);
        setResult({
          kind: 'valid',
          payload,
          serverResult: res.result,
          msg: res.result === 'DUPLICATE'
            ? `Already scanned at ${new Date(res.originalScannedAt ?? res.scannedAt).toLocaleTimeString('en-IN')}`
            : undefined,
        });
        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['manifest', tripId] });
      } catch (e: any) {
        if (e?.status === 0 || e?.code === 'NETWORK_ERROR') {
          await enqueue({
            tripId,
            qrPayload,
            payloadHash: payload.nonce,
            scannedAt,
            localResult: 'VALID',
            serverResult: null,
            serverMessage: null,
            lastSyncAttemptAt: null,
          });
          setResult({ kind: 'queued', payload });
          await refreshCounts();
        } else {
          setResult({ kind: 'invalid', code: e?.code ?? 'UNKNOWN', message: e?.message ?? 'Server error' });
        }
      }
    },
    [tripId, qc, refreshCounts],
  );

  const handleWire = useCallback(async () => {
    const trimmed = wire.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;
    setResult({ kind: 'verifying' });
    if (!cfgQ.data) {
      setResult({ kind: 'invalid', code: 'NO_CONFIG', message: 'Config not loaded â€” retry' });
      return;
    }
    try {
      const payload = await verifyQr(trimmed, cfgQ.data.qrPublicKey, scheduleId);
      if (hasSeenNonce(payload.nonce)) {
        setResult({ kind: 'invalid', code: 'DUPLICATE_LOCAL', message: 'This ticket already scanned this session' });
        return;
      }
      await submitToServer(trimmed, payload);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof QrVerifyError) {
        setResult({ kind: 'invalid', code: e.code, message: e.message });
      } else {
        setResult({ kind: 'invalid', code: 'VERIFY_FAILED', message: String(e) });
      }
    }
  }, [wire, cfgQ.data, scheduleId, submitToServer]);

  const handleManual = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!manualRef.trim() || !manualName.trim()) return;
    setResult({ kind: 'verifying' });
    try {
      const res = await postScan(tripId, {
        scannedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        manualEntry: true,
        bookingReference: manualRef.trim(),
        primaryPassengerName: manualName.trim(),
      });
      setResult({
        kind: 'valid',
        payload: {
          v: 1, ticketId: res.ticketId, bookingId: res.bookingId, scheduleId,
          primarySeatId: '', primarySeatLabel: res.seatLabels[0] ?? '',
          primaryPassengerName: res.primaryPassengerName,
          passengerCount: res.passengerCount, allSeatLabels: res.seatLabels,
          boardingStandId: '', issuedAt: res.scannedAt, nonce: '',
        },
        serverResult: res.result,
      });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['manifest', tripId] });
    } catch (e: any) {
      setResult({ kind: 'invalid', code: e?.code ?? 'MANUAL_FAILED', message: e?.message ?? 'Manual entry failed' });
    }
  }, [manualRef, manualName, tripId, scheduleId, qc]);

  const syncQueue = useCallback(async () => {
    const rows = await listPending(tripId);
    if (rows.length === 0) return;
    for (const row of rows) {
      try {
        const res = await postScan(tripId, { qrPayload: row.qrPayload, scannedAt: row.scannedAt });
        await markSynced(row.id, res.result);
      } catch (e: any) {
        if (e?.status === 0 || e?.code === 'NETWORK_ERROR') {
          break;
        } else {
          await markFailed(row.id, e?.code ?? 'REJECTED', e?.message ?? '');
        }
      }
    }
    await refreshCounts();
  }, [tripId, refreshCounts]);

  return (
    <Screen>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, gap: 16 }}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}>
          <Text style={[styles.back, { color: t.colors.emerald }]}>â† Trip</Text>
        </Pressable>

        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>Scan QR ticket</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.sub, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
          {isWeb
            ? 'Paste the QR payload from the passengerâ€™s ticket'
            : 'Point the camera at the passengerâ€™s ticket'}
        </Text>

        {pendingCount.pending > 0 || pendingCount.failed > 0 ? (
          <Pressable
            onPress={syncQueue}
            style={[styles.queueBar, { backgroundColor: t.colors.surfaceElevated, borderColor: t.colors.warning }]}
          >
            <Text style={{ color: t.colors.warning, fontWeight: '700' }}>
              {pendingCount.pending} pending Â· {pendingCount.failed} failed â€” tap to sync
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        ) : null}

        {result.kind === 'idle' || result.kind === 'verifying' ? (
          <>
            <Text style={[styles.label, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>QR payload</Text>
            <TextInput
              value={wire}
              onChangeText={setWire}
              placeholder="v1:eyJ...:MEUCIQ..."
              placeholderTextColor={t.colors.textMuted}
              multiline
              autoCapitalize="none"
              autoCorrect={false}
              style={[styles.input, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, color: t.colors.textBright }]}
            />
            <Pressable
              onPress={handleWire}
              disabled={result.kind === 'verifying' || !wire.trim() || !cfgQ.data}
              style={[
                styles.primaryBtn,
                { backgroundColor: t.colors.emerald, opacity: result.kind === 'verifying' || !wire.trim() ? 0.5 : 1 },
              ]}
            >
              {result.kind === 'verifying'
                ? <ActivityIndicator color="#052E16" />
                : <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>Verify & submit</Text>}
            </Pressable>

            <View style={{ height: 1, backgroundColor: t.colors.border, marginVertical: 8 }} />

            <Text style={[styles.subtitle, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>Or manual entry</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.label, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>Booking reference</Text>
            <TextInput
              value={manualRef}
              onChangeText={setManualRef}
              placeholder="BKG-2026-00042"
              placeholderTextColor={t.colors.textMuted}
              autoCapitalize="characters"
              style={[styles.input, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, color: t.colors.textBright }]}
            />
            <Text style={[styles.label, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>Primary passenger name</Text>
            <TextInput
              value={manualName}
              onChangeText={setManualName}
              placeholder="Alice Gupta"
              placeholderTextColor={t.colors.textMuted}
              style={[styles.input, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, color: t.colors.textBright }]}
            />
            <Pressable
              onPress={handleManual}
              disabled={!manualRef.trim() || !manualName.trim()}
              style={[styles.secondaryBtn, { borderColor: t.colors.emerald, opacity: !manualRef.trim() || !manualName.trim() ? 0.5 : 1 }]}
            >
              <Text style={{ color: t.colors.emerald, fontWeight: '700' }}>Submit manually</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </>
        ) : null}

        {result.kind === 'valid' ? (
          <ResultCard
            tone="valid"
            title={result.serverResult === 'DUPLICATE' ? 'Already boarded' : 'Boarded'}
            subtitle={`${result.payload.primaryPassengerName} Â· ${result.payload.passengerCount} passenger(s) Â· seats ${result.payload.allSeatLabels.join(', ')}`}
            footer={result.msg}
            onDismiss={() => { setResult({ kind: 'idle' }); setWire(''); setManualRef(''); setManualName(''); }}
            colors={t.colors}
          />
        ) : null}

        {result.kind === 'queued' ? (
          <ResultCard
            tone="queued"
            title="Queued for sync"
            subtitle={`${result.payload.primaryPassengerName} Â· ${result.payload.passengerCount} passenger(s)`}
            footer="Will be sent when network returns"
            onDismiss={() => { setResult({ kind: 'idle' }); setWire(''); }}
            colors={t.colors}
          />
        ) : null}

        {result.kind === 'invalid' ? (
          <ResultCard
            tone="invalid"
            title="Scan rejected"
            subtitle={result.message}
            footer={`Code: ${result.code}`}
            onDismiss={() => { setResult({ kind: 'idle' }); setWire(''); }}
            colors={t.colors}
          />
        ) : null}
      </ScrollView>
    </Screen>
  );
}

function ResultCard({
  tone, title, subtitle, footer, onDismiss, colors,
}: {
  tone: 'valid' | 'queued' | 'invalid';
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  footer?: string | undefined;
  onDismiss: () => void;
  colors: any;
}) {
  const bg = tone === 'valid' ? colors.emeraldSoft : tone === 'queued' ? 'rgba(37,99,235,0.15)' : 'rgba(244,63,94,0.15)';
  const fg = tone === 'valid' ? colors.emerald : tone === 'queued' ? colors.info : colors.danger;
  return (
    <View style={[styles.resultCard, { backgroundColor: bg, borderColor: fg }]}>
      <Text style={{ color: fg, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '800', marginBottom: 4 }}>{title}</Text>
      <Text style={{ color: colors.textBright, fontSize: 14 }}>{subtitle}</Text>
      {footer ? <Text style={{ color: colors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, marginTop: 8 }}>{footer}</Text> : null}
      <Pressable onPress={onDismiss} style={[styles.dismissBtn, { borderColor: fg }]}>
        <Text style={{ color: fg, fontWeight: '700' }}>Scan next</Text>
      </Pressable>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  back:         { fontWeight: '600', fontSize: 14 },
  title:        { fontSize: 22, fontWeight: '800' },
  sub:          { fontSize: 13 },
  subtitle:     { fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '700' },
  label:        { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '600', marginTop: 4 },
  input:        { borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 10, paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 10, fontSize: 14, minHeight: 60, textAlignVertical: 'top' },
  primaryBtn:   { borderRadius: 10, paddingVertical: 14, alignItems: 'center' },
  primaryBtnText:{ color: '#052E16', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800' },
  secondaryBtn: { borderRadius: 10, paddingVertical: 12, alignItems: 'center', borderWidth: 1 },
  queueBar:     { borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1, padding: 10 },
  resultCard:   { borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 12, padding: 16, gap: 6 },
  dismissBtn:   { marginTop: 12, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1, paddingVertical: 10, alignItems: 'center' },

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Modal, View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../../../theme/tokens';

export function CancelTripModal({
  open, submitting, onClose, onSubmit,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  submitting: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (reason: string) => void;
}) {
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
  const canSubmit = reason.trim().length >= 5;

  return (
    <Modal visible={open} transparent animationType="slide" onRequestClose={onClose}>
      <Pressable style={styles.backdrop} onPress={onClose}>
        <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
          <Pressable style={[styles.sheet, { backgroundColor: t.colors.surfaceElevated }]} onPress={() => {}}>
            <Text style={[styles.title, { color: t.colors.danger }]}>Cancel this trip?</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.warn, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
              All confirmed bookings on this trip will be refunded in full. This cannot be undone.
            </Text>

            <Text style={[styles.label, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>Reason (min 5 chars)</Text>
            <TextInput
              value={reason}
              onChangeText={setReason}
              multiline
              maxLength={500}
              placeholder="e.g. Bus breakdown before departure"
              placeholderTextColor={t.colors.textMuted}
              style={[styles.input, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: t.colors.bg, color: t.colors.textBright }]}
            />

            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginTop: 16 }}>
              <Pressable onPress={onClose} style={[styles.btn, { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: t.colors.border }]}>
                <Text style={{ color: t.colors.textMuted, fontWeight: '700' }}>Keep trip</Text>
              </Pressable>
              <Pressable
                onPress={() => canSubmit && onSubmit(reason.trim())}
                disabled={!canSubmit || submitting}
                style={[styles.btn, { backgroundColor: canSubmit ? t.colors.danger : t.colors.border, opacity: submitting ? 0.6 : 1 }]}
              >
                {submitting ? <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" /> : <Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontWeight: '800' }}>Cancel trip</Text>}
              </Pressable>
            </View>
          </Pressable>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </Pressable>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  backdrop: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)', justifyContent: 'flex-end' },
  sheet:    { borderTopLeftRadius: 20, borderTopRightRadius: 20, padding: 20 },

  btn:      { flex: 1, paddingVertical: 12, borderRadius: 10, alignItems: 'center' },
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Modal, View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../../../theme/tokens';
import type { DelayReason } from '../../../lib/api/types';

const REASONS: { key: DelayReason; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'TRAFFIC',      label: 'Traffic' },
  { key: 'WEATHER',      label: 'Weather' },
  { key: 'BREAKDOWN',    label: 'Breakdown' },
  { key: 'ACCIDENT',     label: 'Accident' },
  { key: 'ROAD_CLOSURE', label: 'Road closure' },
  { key: 'OTHER',        label: 'Other' },
];

export function DelayModal({
  open, submitting, onClose, onSubmit,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  submitting: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (body: { reason: DelayReason; delayMinutes: number; notes?: string }) => void;
}) {
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const [reason, setReason] = useState<DelayReason>('TRAFFIC');
  const [minutes, setMinutes] = useState('15');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');

  const submit = () => {
    const m = parseInt(minutes, 10);
    if (!Number.isFinite(m) || m < 1 || m > 180) return;
    onSubmit({ reason, delayMinutes: m, notes: notes.trim() || undefined });
  };

  return (
    <Modal visible={open} transparent animationType="slide" onRequestClose={onClose}>
      <Pressable style={styles.backdrop} onPress={onClose}>
        <KeyboardAvoidingView behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
          <Pressable style={[styles.sheet, { backgroundColor: t.colors.surfaceElevated }]} onPress={() => {}}>
            <Text style={[styles.title, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>Report delay</Text>

            <Text style={[styles.label, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>Reason</Text>
            <View style={styles.chips}>
              {REASONS.map((r) => (
                <Pressable
                  key={r.key}
                  onPress={() => setReason(r.key)}
                  style={[
                    styles.chip,
                    { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: reason === r.key ? t.colors.emeraldSoft : 'transparent' },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text style={{ color: reason === r.key ? t.colors.emerald : t.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '600' }}>
                    {r.label}
                  </Text>
                </Pressable>
              ))}
            </View>

            <Text style={[styles.label, { color: t.colors.textMuted, marginTop: 12 }]}>Minutes (1â€“180)</Text>
            <TextInput
              value={minutes}
              onChangeText={setMinutes}
              keyboardType="number-pad"
              style={[styles.input, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: t.colors.bg, color: t.colors.textBright }]}
            />

            <Text style={[styles.label, { color: t.colors.textMuted, marginTop: 12 }]}>Notes (optional)</Text>
            <TextInput
              value={notes}
              onChangeText={setNotes}
              multiline
              maxLength={200}
              style={[styles.input, styles.textarea, { borderColor: t.colors.border, backgroundColor: t.colors.bg, color: t.colors.textBright }]}
            />

            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginTop: 16 }}>
              <Pressable onPress={onClose} style={[styles.btn, { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: t.colors.border }]}>
                <Text style={{ color: t.colors.textMuted, fontWeight: '700' }}>Cancel</Text>
              </Pressable>
              <Pressable
                onPress={submit}
                disabled={submitting}
                style={[styles.btn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.emerald, opacity: submitting ? 0.6 : 1 }]}
              >
                {submitting ? <ActivityIndicator color="#052E16" /> : <Text style={{ color: '#052E16', fontWeight: '800' }}>Submit</Text>}
              </Pressable>
            </View>
          </Pressable>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </Pressable>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  backdrop: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)', justifyContent: 'flex-end' },
  sheet:    { borderTopLeftRadius: 20, borderTopRightRadius: 20, padding: 20 },
  title:    { fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '800', marginBottom: 12 },
  label:    { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '600', marginBottom: 6 },
  chips:    { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 },
  chip:     { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1 },
  input:    { borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 10, paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 10, fontSize: 16 },
  textarea: { minHeight: 60, textAlignVertical: 'top' },
  btn:      { flex: 1, paddingVertical: 12, borderRadius: 10, alignItems: 'center' },
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, ScrollView, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, Pressable, Alert } from 'react-native';
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
import { Screen } from '../../../components/ui/Screen';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../../../theme/tokens';
import { getTrip, startTrip, endTrip, reportDelay, cancelTrip } from '../../../lib/api/trips.api';
import { formatIST, formatDuration, formatDistanceKm } from '../../../lib/formatters';
import { DelayModal } from '../components/DelayModal';
import { CancelTripModal } from '../components/CancelTripModal';
import type { MainStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/MainStack';
import type { TripStatus } from '../../../lib/api/types';

type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<MainStackParamList, 'TripDetail'>;

function statusBadge(status: TripStatus, colors: any) {
  switch (status) {
    case 'IN_PROGRESS': return { bg: colors.emeraldSoft, fg: colors.emerald };
    case 'COMPLETED':   return { bg: colors.surfaceElevated, fg: colors.textMuted };
    case 'CANCELLED':   return { bg: 'rgba(244,63,94,0.15)', fg: colors.danger };
    default:            return { bg: colors.emeraldSoft, fg: colors.emeraldBright };
  }
}

function Row({ label, value, colors }: { label: string; value: string; colors: any }) {
  return (
    <View style={styles.row}>
      <Text style={[styles.rowLabel, { color: colors.textMuted }]}>{label}</Text>
      <Text style={[styles.rowValue, { color: colors.textBright }]}>{value}</Text>
    </View>
  );
}

export function TripDetailScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
  const { tripId } = route.params;
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [showDelay, setShowDelay] = useState(false);
  const [showCancel, setShowCancel] = useState(false);

  const q = useQuery({ queryKey: ['trip', tripId], queryFn: () => getTrip(tripId) });

  const invalidate = () => {
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['trip', tripId] });
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['trips'] });
  };

  const startMut = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => startTrip(tripId),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
    onError: (e: any) => Alert.alert('Could not start trip', e?.message ?? 'Unknown error'),
  });

  const endMut = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => endTrip(tripId),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
    onError: (e: any) => Alert.alert('Could not end trip', e?.message ?? 'Unknown error'),
  });

  const delayMut = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: { reason: any; delayMinutes: number; notes?: string }) =>
      reportDelay(tripId, body),
    onSuccess: () => { setShowDelay(false); invalidate(); },
    onError: (e: any) => Alert.alert('Could not report delay', e?.message ?? 'Unknown error'),
  });

  const cancelMut = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (reason: string) => cancelTrip(tripId, reason),
    onSuccess: (r) => {
      setShowCancel(false);
      invalidate();
      Alert.alert('Trip cancelled', `${r.cancelledBookings} booking(s) refunded`);
    },
    onError: (e: any) => Alert.alert('Could not cancel trip', e?.message ?? 'Unknown error'),
  });

  if (q.isLoading) {
    return <Screen><View style={styles.center}><ActivityIndicator size="large" color={t.colors.emerald} /></View></Screen>;
  }
  if (q.isError || !q.data) {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <View style={styles.center}>
          <Text style={{ color: t.colors.danger, marginBottom: 12 }}>Could not load trip</Text>
          <Pressable onPress={() => q.refetch()} style={[styles.retryBtn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.emerald }]}>
            <Text style={styles.retryText}>Retry</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </Screen>
    );
  }

  const trip = q.data;
  const badge = statusBadge(trip.status, t.colors);
  const canStart  = trip.status === 'SCHEDULED';
  const canEnd    = trip.status === 'IN_PROGRESS';
  const canDelay  = trip.status === 'IN_PROGRESS';
  const canCancel = trip.status === 'SCHEDULED' || trip.status === 'IN_PROGRESS';
  const canScan   = trip.status === 'SCHEDULED' || trip.status === 'IN_PROGRESS';
  const canManifest = trip.status === 'SCHEDULED' || trip.status === 'IN_PROGRESS';

  const durationMin = trip.startedAt
    ? Math.round(
        ((trip.endedAt ? new Date(trip.endedAt).getTime() : Date.now()) -
          new Date(trip.startedAt).getTime()) / 60000,
      )
    : null;

  return (
    <Screen>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, gap: 16 }}>
        <Pressable onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}>
          <Text style={[styles.back, { color: t.colors.emerald }]}>â† Today's trips</Text>
        </Pressable>

        <View>
          <Text style={[styles.route, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>
            {trip.route.originCity} â†’ {trip.route.destinationCity}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[styles.sub, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
            {trip.bus.model ?? 'Bus'} Â· {trip.bus.registrationNumber}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row' }}>
          <View style={[styles.badge, { backgroundColor: badge.bg }]}>
            <Text style={[styles.badgeText, { color: badge.fg }]}>{trip.status.replace('_', ' ')}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {canScan || canManifest ? (
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
            {canScan ? (
              <Pressable
                onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Scan', { tripId, scheduleId: trip.scheduleId })}
                style={({ pressed }) => [styles.actionBtn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.emerald, opacity: pressed ? 0.9 : 1 }]}
              >
                <Text style={styles.actionBtnText}>Scan QR</Text>
              </Pressable>
            ) : null}
            {canManifest ? (
              <Pressable
                onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Manifest', { tripId })}
                style={({ pressed }) => [styles.actionBtn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.surfaceElevated, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: t.colors.emerald, opacity: pressed ? 0.9 : 1 }]}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.actionBtnText, { color: t.colors.emerald }]}>Manifest</Text>
              </Pressable>
            ) : null}
          </View>
        ) : null}

        <View style={[styles.section, { backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, borderColor: t.colors.border }]}>
          <Row label="Departure" value={formatIST(trip.departureTime, { date: true })} colors={t.colors} />
          <Row label="Arrival"   value={formatIST(trip.arrivalTime, { date: true })} colors={t.colors} />
          <Row label="Boarded"   value={`${trip.boardedCount} / ${trip.confirmedCount}`} colors={t.colors} />
          {trip.startedAt && <Row label="Started" value={formatIST(trip.startedAt, { date: true })} colors={t.colors} />}
          {trip.endedAt && <Row label="Ended" value={formatIST(trip.endedAt, { date: true })} colors={t.colors} />}
          {durationMin != null && <Row label="Duration" value={formatDuration(durationMin)} colors={t.colors} />}
          {trip.distanceKm != null && <Row label="Distance" value={formatDistanceKm(trip.distanceKm)} colors={t.colors} />}
          {trip.delayReason && <Row label="Delay" value={`${trip.delayReason} Â· ${trip.delayMinutes} min`} colors={t.colors} />}
        </View>

        <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>Boarding stands</Text>
        <View style={[styles.section, { backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, borderColor: t.colors.border }]}>
          {trip.boardingStands.map((s) => (
            <Row key={s.standId} label={`#${s.sequence}`} value={s.standName} colors={t.colors} />
          ))}
        </View>

        {canStart && (
          <Pressable
            onPress={() => startMut.mutate()}
            disabled={startMut.isPending}
            style={({ pressed }) => [styles.primaryBtn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.emerald, opacity: startMut.isPending ? 0.6 : pressed ? 0.9 : 1 }]}
          >
            {startMut.isPending ? <ActivityIndicator color="#052E16" /> : <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>Start trip</Text>}
          </Pressable>
        )}

        {canDelay && (
          <Pressable
            onPress={() => setShowDelay(true)}
            style={({ pressed }) => [styles.secondaryBtn, { borderColor: t.colors.emerald, opacity: pressed ? 0.9 : 1 }]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.secondaryBtnText, { color: t.colors.emerald }]}>Report delay</Text>
          </Pressable>
        )}

        {canEnd && (
          <Pressable
            onPress={() =>
              Alert.alert('End this trip?', 'You will be marked off-duty.', [
                { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
                { text: 'End trip', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => endMut.mutate() },
              ])
            }
            disabled={endMut.isPending}
            style={({ pressed }) => [styles.dangerBtn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.danger, opacity: endMut.isPending ? 0.6 : pressed ? 0.9 : 1 }]}
          >
            {endMut.isPending ? <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" /> : <Text style={styles.dangerBtnText}>End trip</Text>}
          </Pressable>
        )}

        {canCancel && (
          <Pressable onPress={() => setShowCancel(true)} style={{ marginTop: 8, paddingVertical: 10, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <Text style={{ color: t.colors.textMuted, fontSize: 13 }}>Cancel this trip</Text>
          </Pressable>
        )}
      </ScrollView>

      <DelayModal
        open={showDelay}
        submitting={delayMut.isPending}
        onClose={() => setShowDelay(false)}
        onSubmit={(body) => delayMut.mutate(body)}
      />

      <CancelTripModal
        open={showCancel}
        submitting={cancelMut.isPending}
        onClose={() => setShowCancel(false)}
        onSubmit={(reason) => cancelMut.mutate(reason)}
      />
    </Screen>
  );
}

  center:         { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: 24 },
  back:           { fontWeight: '600', fontSize: 14 },
  route:          { fontSize: 20, fontWeight: '800' },
  sub:            { fontSize: 13, marginTop: 2 },
  badge:          { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 5, borderRadius: 6 },

function statusStyle(s: TripStatus, t: ReturnType<typeof useDriverTheme>) {
  switch (s) {
    case 'IN_PROGRESS': return { bg: t.colors.emeraldSoft, fg: t.colors.emerald };
    case 'COMPLETED':   return { bg: t.colors.surfaceElevated, fg: t.colors.textMuted };
    case 'CANCELLED':   return { bg: 'rgba(244,63,94,0.15)', fg: t.colors.danger };
    default:            return { bg: t.colors.emeraldSoft, fg: t.colors.emeraldBright };
  }
}

function TripCard({ trip, onPress }: { trip: DriverTripSummary; onPress: () => void }) {
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const s = statusStyle(trip.status, t);
  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={onPress}
      style={({ pressed }) => [
        styles.card,
        { backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, borderColor: t.colors.border, opacity: pressed ? 0.85 : 1 },
      ]}
    >
      <Text style={[styles.route, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>
        {trip.route.originCity} â†’ {trip.route.destinationCity}
      </Text>
      <Text style={[styles.sub, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
        {trip.bus.model ?? 'Bus'} Â· {trip.bus.registrationNumber}
      </Text>
      <Text style={[styles.sub, { color: t.colors.textSecondary }]}>
        {formatIST(trip.departureTime)} â†’ {formatIST(trip.arrivalTime)}
      </Text>
      <View style={styles.row}>
        <View style={[styles.badge, { backgroundColor: s.bg }]}>
          <Text style={[styles.badgeText, { color: s.fg }]}>{trip.status.replace('_', ' ')}</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={[styles.counts, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
          Boarded {trip.boardedCount}/{trip.confirmedCount}
        </Text>
      </View>
    </Pressable>
  );
}

export function TripsListScreen() {

            style={[styles.logoutBtn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.surfaceElevated, borderColor: t.colors.border }]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.logoutText, { color: t.colors.textBright }]}>Log out</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </View>

      {q.isLoading ? (
        <View style={styles.center}><ActivityIndicator color={t.colors.emerald} size="large" /></View>
      ) : q.isError ? (
        <View style={styles.center}>
          <Text style={{ color: t.colors.danger, marginBottom: 12 }}>Could not load trips</Text>
          <Pressable onPress={() => q.refetch()} style={[styles.retryBtn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.emerald }]}>
            <Text style={styles.retryText}>Retry</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      ) : (q.data?.length ?? 0) === 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.center}>
          <Text style={[styles.subtle, { color: t.colors.textMuted, textAlign: 'center' }]}>
            No trips assigned today
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          data={q.data}
          keyExtractor={(it) => it.tripId}
          contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, gap: 12 }}
          renderItem={({ item }) => (
            <TripCard trip={item} onPress={() => nav.navigate('TripDetail', { tripId: item.tripId })} />
          )}
          refreshControl={
            <RefreshControl refreshing={q.isFetching} onRefresh={() => q.refetch()} tintColor={t.colors.emerald} />
          }
        />
      )}
    </Screen>
  );
}

import { api } from './client';
import { AuthTokens, DriverConfig, DriverMe } from './types';

export async function requestOtp(phone: string): Promise<{ expiresInSeconds: number }> {
  const res = await api.post<{ expiresInSeconds: number }>(
    '/driver/auth/otp/request',
    { phone },
    { skipAuth: true },
  );
  return res.data;
}

export async function verifyOtp(params: {
  phone: string;
  otp: string;
  deviceFingerprint: string;
}): Promise<AuthTokens> {
  const res = await api.post<AuthTokens>(
    '/driver/auth/otp/verify',
    params,
    { skipAuth: true },
  );
  return res.data;
}

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import Constants from 'expo-constants';

// Web: use whatever host the browser is on (localhost / 127.0.0.1 / LAN IP).
// Native: use the same IP the phone already uses to reach Metro, port 4310.
//         Derived from Expo's hostUri â€” auto-detects physical device IP,
//         emulator IP, or tunnel domain. No .env edit needed.
function resolveBaseUrl(): string {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.location) {
      const proto = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'https' : 'http';
      const host = window.location.hostname || 'localhost';
      return `${proto}://${host}:4310/v1`;
    }
    return 'http://localhost:4310/v1';
  }

  const hostUri =
    (Constants.expoConfig as any)?.hostUri ??
    (Constants.expoGoConfig as any)?.debuggerHost ??
    null;

  if (hostUri && typeof hostUri === 'string') {
    const host = hostUri.split(':')[0];
    return `http://${host}:4310/v1`;
  }

  return process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE ?? 'http://10.0.2.2:4310/v1';
}

export const API_BASE = resolveBaseUrl();
console.log('[DRIVER] API_BASE = ' + API_BASE);

export class ApiError extends Error {
  code: string;
  status: number;
  constructor(code: string, message: string, status: number) {
    super(message);
    this.code = code;
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export interface ApiOptions {
  method?: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE';
  body?: unknown;
  token?: string;
}

export async function apiCall<T>(path: string, opts: ApiOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
  const url = API_BASE + (path.startsWith('/') ? path : '/' + path);
  const method = opts.method ?? 'GET';
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
  if (opts.token) headers.Authorization = 'Bearer ' + opts.token;

  console.log('[API] ->', method, url);
  const started = Date.now();
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 15000);

  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await fetch(url, {
      method,
      headers,
      body: opts.body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(opts.body) : undefined,
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    const ms = Date.now() - started;
    console.error('[API] NETWORK FAIL', method, url, 'after', ms + 'ms', '->', e?.name, e?.message);
    if (e?.name === 'AbortError') {
      throw new ApiError('NETWORK_TIMEOUT', 'Timeout after 15s (' + API_BASE + ')', 0);
    }
    throw new ApiError('NETWORK_ERROR', 'Cannot reach ' + API_BASE + ' - ' + (e?.message ?? 'network error'), 0);
  }
  clearTimeout(timer);

  const ms = Date.now() - started;
  const isJson = (res.headers.get('content-type') ?? '').includes('application/json');
  const body: any = isJson ? await res.json().catch(() => ({})) : {};

  if (!res.ok) {
    const code = body?.error?.code ?? 'HTTP_ERROR';
    const message = body?.error?.message ?? res.statusText ?? ('HTTP ' + res.status);
    console.error('[API] <-', res.status, method, url, 'in', ms + 'ms', '->', code, message);
    throw new ApiError(code, message, res.status);
  }

  console.log('[API] <-', res.status, method, url, 'in', ms + 'ms');
  return (body?.data !== undefined ? body.data : body) as T;
}

FILE: apps\driver-app\src\lib\api\client.ts
SIZE: 4352 bytes
================================================================================
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { ApiError, ApiErrorResponse, ApiResponse } from './types';

// Web is ALWAYS the same machine as the backend. Hardcode it â€” no env-var
// substitution cache can override this. If you need to test web against a
// remote backend, edit the string below.
const WEB_API_BASE = 'http://localhost:4310/v1';

// Native (Android/iOS) must use the laptop LAN IP. Read from .env.
const NATIVE_API_BASE =
  process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE ?? 'http://10.0.2.2:4310/v1';

const API_BASE = Platform.OS === 'web' ? WEB_API_BASE : NATIVE_API_BASE;

// CANARY â€” this appears in browser console on every app load.
// If you see 'http://localhost:4310/v1' here, the bundle is FRESH.
// If you see anything else, an old bundle is still cached.
console.log('[api] BOOT  platform=', Platform.OS, ' base=', API_BASE);

const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;

type Method = 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE';
interface RequestOptions {
  method?: Method; body?: unknown; headers?: Record<string, string>;
  skipAuth?: boolean; idempotencyKey?: string;
}
type RefreshHandler = () => Promise<string | null>;

class ApiClient {
  private accessToken: string | null = null;
  private refreshHandler: RefreshHandler | null = null;
  get baseUrl(): string { return API_BASE; }
  setAccessToken(t: string | null): void { this.accessToken = t; }
  setRefreshHandler(f: RefreshHandler | null): void { this.refreshHandler = f; }

  async request<T>(path: string, opts: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<ApiResponse<T>> {
    const url = API_BASE + (path.startsWith('/') ? path : '/' + path);
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...opts.headers };
    if (!opts.skipAuth && this.accessToken) headers.Authorization = 'Bearer ' + this.accessToken;
    if (opts.idempotencyKey) headers['Idempotency-Key'] = opts.idempotencyKey;

    const init: RequestInit = { method: opts.method ?? 'GET', headers };
    if (opts.body !== undefined && init.method !== 'GET') init.body = JSON.stringify(opts.body);

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
    init.signal = controller.signal;

    if (__DEV__) console.log('[api] â†’', init.method, url);

    let response: Response;
    try {
      response = await fetch(url, init);
    } catch (e: any) {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      if (e?.name === 'AbortError') {
        if (__DEV__) console.warn('[api] TIMEOUT', url);
        throw new ApiError(0, { code: 'NETWORK_TIMEOUT', message: `Timeout after ${REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000}s (${API_BASE})` });
      }
      if (__DEV__) console.warn('[api] NETWORK ERROR', url, e?.message);
      throw new ApiError(0, { code: 'NETWORK_ERROR', message: `Cannot reach ${API_BASE} â€” ${e?.message ?? 'network error'}` });
    }
    clearTimeout(timer);

    if (__DEV__) console.log('[api] â†', response.status, url);

    if (response.status === 401 && !opts.skipAuth && this.refreshHandler) {
      const fresh = await this.refreshHandler();
      if (fresh) {
        headers.Authorization = 'Bearer ' + fresh;
        response = await fetch(url, { ...init, headers });
      }
    }
    return this.parse<T>(response);
  }

  private async parse<T>(response: Response): Promise<ApiResponse<T>> {
    const isJson = (response.headers.get('content-type') ?? '').includes('application/json');
    if (!response.ok) {
      if (isJson) {
        const body = (await response.json()) as ApiErrorResponse;
        if (__DEV__) console.warn('[api] ERROR', response.status, body);
        if (body?.error) throw new ApiError(response.status, body.error);
      }
      throw new ApiError(response.status, { code: 'HTTP_ERROR', message: response.statusText || `HTTP ${response.status}` });
    }
    if (response.status === 204) return { data: {} as T };
    return (await response.json()) as ApiResponse<T>;
  }

  get<T>(p: string) { return this.request<T>(p, { method: 'GET' }); }
  post<T>(p: string, b?: unknown, e: Partial<RequestOptions> = {}) { return this.request<T>(p, { method: 'POST', body: b, ...e }); }
  patch<T>(p: string, b?: unknown) { return this.request<T>(p, { method: 'PATCH', body: b }); }
  delete<T>(p: string) { return this.request<T>(p, { method: 'DELETE' }); }
}

export const api = new ApiClient();

FILE: apps\driver-app\src\lib\auth\AuthContext.tsx
SIZE: 3548 bytes
