import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { UserSession } from '../api/types';
import * as authApi from '../api/auth.api';
import * as store from './secureStore';
import { getDeviceFingerprint } from './deviceFingerprint';

interface AuthCtx {
  user: UserSession | null;
  isLoading: boolean;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  requestOtp: (phone: string) => Promise<void>;
  verifyOtp: (phone: string, otp: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
}

const Ctx = createContext<AuthCtx | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserSession | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  const applyTokens = useCallback(async (tokens: { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string; user: UserSession }) => {
    await store.saveTokens(tokens.accessToken, tokens.refreshToken);
    api.setAccessToken(tokens.accessToken);
    setUser(tokens.user);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;
    (async () => {
      try {
        const { access, refresh } = await store.loadTokens();
        if (access) {
          api.setAccessToken(access);
          // Try a cheap authenticated call to validate the token
          try {
            const me = await authApi.fetchDriverMe();
            if (mounted) setUser({ id: me.userId, phone: me.phone, name: me.name });
          } catch {
            // Access token invalid; try refresh
            if (refresh) {
              try {
                const t = await authApi.refreshTokens(refresh);
                await applyTokens(t);
              } catch {
                await store.clearTokens();
                api.setAccessToken(null);
              }
            } else {
              await store.clearTokens();
              api.setAccessToken(null);
            }
          }
        }
      } finally {
        if (mounted) setIsLoading(false);
      }
    })();

    api.setRefreshHandler(async () => {
      const { refresh } = await store.loadTokens();
      if (!refresh) return null;
      try {
        const t = await authApi.refreshTokens(refresh);
        await store.saveTokens(t.accessToken, t.refreshToken);
        api.setAccessToken(t.accessToken);
        return t.accessToken;
      } catch {
        await store.clearTokens();
        api.setAccessToken(null);
        setUser(null);
        return null;
      }
    });

    return () => { mounted = false; };
  }, [applyTokens]);

  const requestOtp = useCallback(async (phone: string) => {
    await authApi.requestOtp(phone);
  }, []);

  const verifyOtp = useCallback(async (phone: string, otp: string) => {
    const fingerprint = await getDeviceFingerprint();
    const tokens = await authApi.verifyOtp({ phone, otp, deviceFingerprint: fingerprint });
    await applyTokens(tokens);
  }, [applyTokens]);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    try { await authApi.logout(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    await store.clearTokens();
    api.setAccessToken(null);
    setUser(null);
  }, []);

  return (
    <Ctx.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        isLoading,
        isAuthenticated: !!user,
        requestOtp,
        verifyOtp,
        logout,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </Ctx.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth(): AuthCtx {
  const ctx = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider');
  return ctx;
}

import * as Application from 'expo-application';
import * as Device from 'expo-device';
import * as Crypto from 'expo-crypto';
import { env } from '../../config/env';

/**
 * Stable per-device identifier. Server binds the driver session to this hash
 * for single-device enforcement (buscon/02 Â§5.2).
 */
export async function getDeviceFingerprint(): Promise<string> {
  const parts = [
    Application.applicationId ?? 'app',
    Application.nativeApplicationVersion ?? '0',
    Device.modelName ?? 'device',
    Device.osName ?? 'os',
    Device.osVersion ?? '0',
    env.APP_VERSION,
  ].join('|');
  return await Crypto.digestStringAsync(Crypto.CryptoDigestAlgorithm.SHA256, parts);
}

import { Platform } from 'react-native';

const DEVICE_KEY = 'busverse_device_id';

async function getOrCreateDeviceId(): Promise<string> {
  let id: string | null = null;
  try {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      id = localStorage.getItem(DEVICE_KEY);
    } else {
      const SS = await import('expo-secure-store');
      id = await SS.getItemAsync(DEVICE_KEY);
    }
  } catch { /* ignore */ }

  if (!id) {
    id = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2) + Date.now().toString(36);
    try {
      if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
        localStorage.setItem(DEVICE_KEY, id);
      } else {
        const SS = await import('expo-secure-store');
        await SS.setItemAsync(DEVICE_KEY, id);
      }
    } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }
  return id;
}

export async function getDeviceFingerprint(): Promise<string> {
  const id = await getOrCreateDeviceId();
  const parts = [Platform.OS, 'busverse-driver', '1.0.0', id].join('|');

  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.subtle) {
    const buf = new TextEncoder().encode(parts);
    const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', buf);
    return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hash)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  }

  const Crypto = await import('expo-crypto');
  return Crypto.digestStringAsync(Crypto.CryptoDigestAlgorithm.SHA256, parts);
}

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';

const ACCESS_KEY  = 'busverse_driver_access';
const REFRESH_KEY = 'busverse_driver_refresh';
const isWeb = Platform.OS === 'web';

async function setItem(key: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
  if (isWeb) { localStorage.setItem(key, value); return; }
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(key, value);
}

async function getItem(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
  if (isWeb) return localStorage.getItem(key);
  return await SecureStore.getItemAsync(key);
}

async function deleteItem(key: string): Promise<void> {
  if (isWeb) { localStorage.removeItem(key); return; }
  await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(key);
}

export async function saveTokens(access: string, refresh: string): Promise<void> {
  await Promise.all([setItem(ACCESS_KEY, access), setItem(REFRESH_KEY, refresh)]);
}

export async function loadTokens(): Promise<{ access: string | null; refresh: string | null }> {
  const [access, refresh] = await Promise.all([getItem(ACCESS_KEY), getItem(REFRESH_KEY)]);
  return { access, refresh };
}

export async function clearTokens(): Promise<void> {
  await Promise.all([deleteItem(ACCESS_KEY), deleteItem(REFRESH_KEY)]);
}

import { Platform } from 'react-native';

const SESSION_KEY = 'busverse_driver_session';

export interface StoredSession {
  token: string;
  user: { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string };
}

export async function saveSession(session: StoredSession): Promise<void> {
  const json = JSON.stringify(session);
  if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
    localStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, json);
    return;
  }
  const SS = await import('expo-secure-store');
  await SS.setItemAsync(SESSION_KEY, json);
}

export async function loadSession(): Promise<StoredSession | null> {
  let json: string | null = null;
  try {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      json = localStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY);
    } else {
      const SS = await import('expo-secure-store');
      json = await SS.getItemAsync(SESSION_KEY);
    }
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  if (!json) return null;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(json) as StoredSession;
    if (!parsed?.token || !parsed?.user) return null;
    return parsed;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function clearSession(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
      return;
    }
    const SS = await import('expo-secure-store');
    await SS.deleteItemAsync(SESSION_KEY);
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

export function formatIST(iso: string, opts?: { date?: boolean }): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const time = d.toLocaleString('en-IN', {
    timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
    hour12: false,
  });
  if (!opts?.date) return time + ' IST';
  const date = d.toLocaleString('en-IN', {
    timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
    weekday: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
  });
  return date + ' Â· ' + time + ' IST';
}

export function formatDuration(minutes: number): string {
  const h = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  const m = minutes % 60;
  return h > 0 ? h + 'h ' + m + 'm' : m + 'm';
}

export function formatDistanceKm(km: number): string {
  return km.toFixed(1) + ' km';
}

export function formatINR(paise: number): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-IN', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'INR',
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(paise / 100);
}

FILE: apps\driver-app\src\lib\qr\nonceCache.ts

/**
 * In-memory anti-replay. Prevents double-scanning the same QR in one session.
 * Server's boarding_scans (ticket_id, trip_id) unique constraint is the
 * real guarantee â€” this is just UX (avoid pointless server round-trips).
 */
const seen = new Set<string>();
const MAX = 200;

export function hasSeenNonce(nonce: string): boolean {
  return seen.has(nonce);
}

export function recordNonce(nonce: string): void {
  seen.add(nonce);
  if (seen.size > MAX) {
    const first = seen.values().next().value;
    if (first) seen.delete(first);
  }
}

// Mirrors apps/api/src/lib/qr.ts QRPayloadV2 exactly.
// 12 canonical fields (sorted at sign time, do NOT reorder â€” verify uses byte equality).
export interface QRPayloadV2 {
  v: 1;
  ticketId: string;
  bookingId: string;
  scheduleId: string;
  primarySeatId: string;
  primarySeatLabel: string;
  primaryPassengerName: string;
  passengerCount: number;
  allSeatLabels: string[];
  boardingStandId: string;
  issuedAt: string;
  nonce: string;
}

export class QrVerifyError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly code:
      | 'INVALID_FORMAT'
      | 'UNSUPPORTED_WIRE_VERSION'
      | 'INVALID_SIGNATURE'
      | 'INVALID_SIGNATURE_LENGTH'
      | 'INVALID_PAYLOAD'
      | 'UNSUPPORTED_PAYLOAD_VERSION'
      | 'INVALID_TRIP'
      | 'SHA512_NOT_AVAILABLE',
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'QrVerifyError';
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps\driver-app\src\lib\qr\verify.ts
SIZE: 3250 bytes
================================================================================
import * as ed from '@noble/ed25519';
import { sha512 } from '@noble/hashes/sha2.js';
import { QRPayloadV2, QrVerifyError } from './types';

// @noble/hashes v2.x: sha512 lives in sha2.js (the /sha512 subpath was removed).
// @noble/ed25519 v2.x: set both sync and async on `ed.etc`.
const etc = (ed as any).etc;
if (etc) {
  if (!etc.sha512Sync) {
    etc.sha512Sync = (...m: Uint8Array[]) => sha512(etc.concatBytes(...m));
  }
  if (!etc.sha512Async) {
    etc.sha512Async = async (...m: Uint8Array[]) => sha512(etc.concatBytes(...m));
  }
}

function base64ToBytes(b64: string): Uint8Array {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return bytes;
}

function bytesToText(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  if (typeof TextDecoder !== 'undefined') return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
  let out = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) out += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]!);
  return decodeURIComponent(escape(out));
}

export interface ParsedQr {
  payload: QRPayloadV2;
  signature: Uint8Array;
  canonicalBytes: Uint8Array;
}

export function parseWire(wire: string): ParsedQr {
  const parts = wire.split(':');
  if (parts.length !== 3) throw new QrVerifyError('INVALID_FORMAT', 'QR must be v1:payload:sig');
  if (parts[0] !== 'v1') throw new QrVerifyError('UNSUPPORTED_WIRE_VERSION', `Wire version ${parts[0]} not supported`);

  let canonicalBytes: Uint8Array;
  let signature: Uint8Array;
  try {
    canonicalBytes = base64ToBytes(parts[1]!);
    signature = base64ToBytes(parts[2]!);
  } catch {
    throw new QrVerifyError('INVALID_FORMAT', 'Base64 decode failed');
  }
  if (signature.length !== 64) throw new QrVerifyError('INVALID_SIGNATURE_LENGTH', 'Signature must be 64 bytes');

  let payload: QRPayloadV2;
  try {
    payload = JSON.parse(bytesToText(canonicalBytes)) as QRPayloadV2;
  } catch {
    throw new QrVerifyError('INVALID_PAYLOAD', 'Payload is not valid JSON');
  }
  if (payload.v !== 1) throw new QrVerifyError('UNSUPPORTED_PAYLOAD_VERSION', `Payload version ${payload.v}`);

  return { payload, signature, canonicalBytes };
}

export async function verifyQr(
  wire: string,
  publicKeyB64: string,
  currentTripScheduleId: string,
): Promise<QRPayloadV2> {
  let parsed: ParsedQr;
  try {
    parsed = parseWire(wire);
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof QrVerifyError) throw e;
    throw new QrVerifyError('INVALID_FORMAT', 'Parse failed');
  }

  const publicKey = base64ToBytes(publicKeyB64);
  if (publicKey.length !== 32) throw new QrVerifyError('INVALID_SIGNATURE', 'Public key must be 32 bytes');

  let ok: boolean;
  try {
    ok = await (ed as any).verifyAsync(parsed.signature, parsed.canonicalBytes, publicKey);
  } catch (e) {
    const msg = String(e);
    if (msg.includes('sha512')) throw new QrVerifyError('SHA512_NOT_AVAILABLE', 'Ed25519 sha512 not available');
    throw new QrVerifyError('INVALID_SIGNATURE', 'Verify failed');
  }
  if (!ok) throw new QrVerifyError('INVALID_SIGNATURE', 'Signature does not match');

  if (parsed.payload.scheduleId !== currentTripScheduleId) {
    throw new QrVerifyError('INVALID_TRIP', 'Ticket is for a different schedule');
  }
  return parsed.payload;
}

FILE: apps\driver-app\src\lib\scan\queue.ts
SIZE: 6491 bytes
================================================================================
import { Platform } from 'react-native';

export interface PendingScan {
  id: string;
  tripId: string;
  qrPayload: string;
  payloadHash: string;
  scannedAt: string;
  localResult: string;
  syncStatus: 'PENDING' | 'SYNCED' | 'FAILED';
  syncAttempts: number;
  serverResult: string | null;
  serverMessage: string | null;
  lastSyncAttemptAt: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
}

const WEB_KEY = 'busverse_driver_pending_scans';
const isWeb = Platform.OS === 'web';

// ============================================================
// Web â€” localStorage
// ============================================================
function webLoad(): PendingScan[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(WEB_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
  } catch { return []; }
}

function webSave(all: PendingScan[]): void {
  try { localStorage.setItem(WEB_KEY, JSON.stringify(all)); } catch { /* quota */ }
}

// ============================================================
// Native â€” SQLite (lazy init)
// ============================================================
let sqliteDb: any = null;

async function getDb(): Promise<any> {
  if (sqliteDb) return sqliteDb;
  const SQLite = await import('expo-sqlite');
  sqliteDb = await SQLite.openDatabaseAsync('busverse_driver.db');
  await sqliteDb.execAsync(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pending_scans (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      trip_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      qr_payload TEXT NOT NULL,
      payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
      scanned_at TEXT NOT NULL,
      local_result TEXT NOT NULL,
      sync_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
      sync_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      server_result TEXT,
      server_message TEXT,
      last_sync_attempt_at TEXT,
      created_at TEXT NOT NULL
    );
    CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uniq_trip_payload ON pending_scans(trip_id, payload_hash);
    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_status ON pending_scans(sync_status);
  `);
  return sqliteDb;
}

// ============================================================
// Public API
// ============================================================

export async function enqueue(scan: Omit<PendingScan, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'syncStatus' | 'syncAttempts'>): Promise<PendingScan> {
  const full: PendingScan = {
    ...scan,
    id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
    syncStatus: 'PENDING',
    syncAttempts: 0,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  if (isWeb) {
    const all = webLoad();
    if (all.find((s) => s.tripId === full.tripId && s.payloadHash === full.payloadHash)) {
      return all.find((s) => s.tripId === full.tripId && s.payloadHash === full.payloadHash)!;
    }
    all.push(full);
    webSave(all);
    return full;
  }
  const db = await getDb();
  try {
    await db.runAsync(
      `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO pending_scans
         (id, trip_id, qr_payload, payload_hash, scanned_at, local_result,
          sync_status, sync_attempts, server_result, server_message,
          last_sync_attempt_at, created_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      full.id, full.tripId, full.qrPayload, full.payloadHash, full.scannedAt,
      full.localResult, full.syncStatus, full.syncAttempts, null, null, null, full.createdAt,
    );
    const row = await db.getFirstAsync(
      'SELECT * FROM pending_scans WHERE trip_id = ? AND payload_hash = ?',
      full.tripId, full.payloadHash,
    );
    return rowToScan(row);
  } catch {
    return full;
  }
}

export async function listPending(tripId: string): Promise<PendingScan[]> {
  if (isWeb) {
    return webLoad().filter((s) => s.tripId === tripId && s.syncStatus === 'PENDING');
  }
  const db = await getDb();
  const rows = await db.getAllAsync(
    'SELECT * FROM pending_scans WHERE trip_id = ? AND sync_status = ? ORDER BY scanned_at ASC',
    tripId, 'PENDING',
  );
  return rows.map(rowToScan);
}

export async function countPending(tripId: string): Promise<{ pending: number; failed: number }> {
  if (isWeb) {
    const all = webLoad().filter((s) => s.tripId === tripId);
    return {
      pending: all.filter((s) => s.syncStatus === 'PENDING').length,
      failed: all.filter((s) => s.syncStatus === 'FAILED').length,
    };
  }
  const db = await getDb();
  const p = await db.getFirstAsync<{ c: number }>(
    'SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM pending_scans WHERE trip_id = ? AND sync_status = ?', tripId, 'PENDING',
  );
  const f = await db.getFirstAsync<{ c: number }>(
    'SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM pending_scans WHERE trip_id = ? AND sync_status = ?', tripId, 'FAILED',
  );
  return { pending: p?.c ?? 0, failed: f?.c ?? 0 };
}

export async function markSynced(id: string, serverResult: string): Promise<void> {
  if (isWeb) {
    const all = webLoad().map((s) => s.id === id ? { ...s, syncStatus: 'SYNCED' as const, serverResult } : s);
    webSave(all);
    return;
  }
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.runAsync(
    'UPDATE pending_scans SET sync_status = ?, server_result = ?, last_sync_attempt_at = ? WHERE id = ?',
    'SYNCED', serverResult, new Date().toISOString(), id,
  );
}

export async function markFailed(id: string, serverResult: string, message: string): Promise<void> {
  if (isWeb) {
    const all = webLoad().map((s) => s.id === id ? {
      ...s, syncStatus: 'FAILED' as const, serverResult, serverMessage: message,
    } : s);
    webSave(all);
    return;
  }
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.runAsync(
    'UPDATE pending_scans SET sync_status = ?, server_result = ?, server_message = ?, last_sync_attempt_at = ? WHERE id = ?',
    'FAILED', serverResult, message, new Date().toISOString(), id,
  );
}

export async function incrementAttempt(id: string): Promise<void> {
  if (isWeb) {
    const all = webLoad().map((s) => s.id === id ? { ...s, syncAttempts: s.syncAttempts + 1 } : s);
    webSave(all);
    return;
  }
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.runAsync(
    'UPDATE pending_scans SET sync_attempts = sync_attempts + 1, last_sync_attempt_at = ? WHERE id = ?',
    new Date().toISOString(), id,
  );
}

function rowToScan(r: any): PendingScan {
  return {
    id: r.id,
    tripId: r.trip_id,
    qrPayload: r.qr_payload,
    payloadHash: r.payload_hash,
    scannedAt: r.scanned_at,
    localResult: r.local_result,
    syncStatus: r.sync_status,
    syncAttempts: r.sync_attempts,
    serverResult: r.server_result,
    serverMessage: r.server_message,
    lastSyncAttemptAt: r.last_sync_attempt_at,
    createdAt: r.created_at,
  };
}

import React from 'react';
import { View, ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, useColorScheme } from 'react-native';
import { NavigationContainer, DarkTheme as NavDarkTheme, DefaultTheme as NavLightTheme } from '@react-navigation/native';
import { useAuth } from '../lib/auth/AuthContext';
import { AuthStack } from './AuthStack';
import { MainStack } from './MainStack';
import { useDriverTheme } from '../theme/tokens';
import { TransitLoader } from '../components/TransitLoader';

export function RootNavigator() {
  const { isLoading, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const t = useDriverTheme();
  const scheme = useColorScheme();

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.loading, { backgroundColor: t.colors.bg }]}>
        <TransitLoader size={56} label="VERIFYING SESSION" />
      </View>
    );
  }

  const base = scheme === 'light' ? NavLightTheme : NavDarkTheme;
  const navTheme = {
    ...base,
    colors: {
      ...base.colors,
      background: t.colors.bg,
      card:       t.colors.surface,
      text:       t.colors.textBright,
      primary:    t.colors.emerald,
      border:     t.colors.border,
    },
  };

  return (
    <NavigationContainer theme={navTheme}>
      {isAuthenticated ? <MainStack /> : <AuthStack />}
    </NavigationContainer>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  loading: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  Text,
  Modal,
  TouchableOpacity,
  TextInput,
  ScrollView,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';

interface SpotTicketingModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  availableSeats: string[];
  onIssueTicket: (ticket: {
    passengerName: string;
    seatLabel: string;
    boardingStand: string;
    dropStand: string;
    fare: number;
    paymentMode: 'CASH' | 'SPOT_UPI';
  }) => void;
}

export function SpotTicketingModal({
  visible,
  onClose,
  availableSeats,
  onIssueTicket,
}: SpotTicketingModalProps) {
  const [name, setName] = useState('Walk-in Passenger');
  const [selectedSeat, setSelectedSeat] = useState<string>(availableSeats[0] || 'A3');
  const [boardingStand, setBoardingStand] = useState('Unnao Bypass');
  const [dropStand, setDropStand] = useState('Lucknow Alambagh');
  const [fare, setFare] = useState('220');
  const [paymentMode, setPaymentMode] = useState<'CASH' | 'SPOT_UPI'>('CASH');

  const handleIssue = () => {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      Alert.alert('Validation Error', 'Enter passenger name.');
      return;
    }
    if (!selectedSeat) {
      Alert.alert('Validation Error', 'Select an unoccupied seat.');
      return;
    }

    onIssueTicket({
      passengerName: name.trim(),
      seatLabel: selectedSeat,
      boardingStand,
      dropStand,
      fare: parseInt(fare, 10) || 200,
      paymentMode,
    });

    Alert.alert('Ticket Issued', `Seat ${selectedSeat} assigned. Collected â‚¹${fare} via ${paymentMode}.`);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="slide">
      <View style={styles.backdrop}>
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.head}>
            <View>
              <Text style={styles.title}>Spot Ticket Issuance</Text>
              <Text style={styles.sub}>Highway Walk-in Cash / Spot-UPI</Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} style={styles.closeBtn}>
              <Ionicons name="close" size={20} color="#F0FDF4" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 12 }}>
            <View>
              <Text style={styles.label}>PASSENGER NAME</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.input}
                value={name}
                onChangeText={setName}
                placeholderTextColor="#689F88"
              />
            </View>

            <View>
              <Text style={styles.label}>SELECT AVAILABLE SEAT</Text>
              <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={styles.seatRow}>
                {availableSeats.map((seat) => (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={seat}
                    style={[styles.seatPill, selectedSeat === seat && styles.seatPillActive]}
                    onPress={() => setSelectedSeat(seat)}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.seatText, selectedSeat === seat && styles.seatTextActive]}>
                      {seat}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                ))}
              </ScrollView>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.twoCol}>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={styles.label}>BOARDING</Text>
                <TextInput style={styles.input} value={boardingStand} onChangeText={setBoardingStand} />
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={styles.label}>DROPPING</Text>
                <TextInput style={styles.input} value={dropStand} onChangeText={setDropStand} />
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.twoCol}>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={styles.label}>COLLECTION FARE (INR)</Text>
                <TextInput
                  style={[styles.input, { color: '#10B981', fontWeight: '900' }]}
                  value={fare}
                  keyboardType="numeric"
                  onChangeText={setFare}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={styles.label}>PAYMENT MODE</Text>
                <View style={styles.modeRow}>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.modeBtn, paymentMode === 'CASH' && styles.modeBtnActive]}
                    onPress={() => setPaymentMode('CASH')}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.modeText, paymentMode === 'CASH' && styles.modeTextActive]}>CASH</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.modeBtn, paymentMode === 'SPOT_UPI' && styles.modeBtnActive]}
                    onPress={() => setPaymentMode('SPOT_UPI')}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.modeText, paymentMode === 'SPOT_UPI' && styles.modeTextActive]}>UPI</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.issueBtn} onPress={handleIssue} activeOpacity={0.85}>
              <Ionicons name="print-outline" size={18} color="#020604" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
              <Text style={styles.issueBtnText}>ISSUE TICKET & RECORD CASH</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  backdrop: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.8)', justifyContent: 'flex-end' },
  card: { backgroundColor: '#07160F', borderTopLeftRadius: 24, borderTopRightRadius: 24, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#133522', padding: 20, maxHeight: '85%' },
  head: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 12 },
  title: { color: '#F0FDF4', fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '900', fontFamily: 'monospace' },
  sub: { color: '#689F88', fontSize: 11, fontFamily: 'monospace' },
  closeBtn: { padding: 4 },
  label: { color: '#689F88', fontSize: 9.5, fontFamily: 'monospace', fontWeight: 'bold', marginBottom: 4 },
  input: { backgroundColor: '#020604', borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#133522', color: '#F0FDF4', paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 9, fontSize: 13, fontFamily: 'monospace' },
  seatRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, paddingVertical: 4 },
  seatPill: { paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 8, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: '#020604', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#133522' },
  seatPillActive: { backgroundColor: '#10B981', borderColor: '#10B981' },
  seatText: { color: '#86EFAC', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 'bold', fontFamily: 'monospace' },
  seatTextActive: { color: '#020604' },
  twoCol: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 },
  modeRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 },
  modeBtn: { flex: 1, paddingVertical: 10, borderRadius: 10, backgroundColor: '#020604', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#133522', alignItems: 'center' },
  modeBtnActive: { backgroundColor: '#10B981', borderColor: '#10B981' },
  modeText: { color: '#86EFAC', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', fontFamily: 'monospace' },
  modeTextActive: { color: '#020604' },
  issueBtn: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', backgroundColor: '#10B981', paddingVertical: 14, borderRadius: 12, marginTop: 8 },
  issueBtnText: { color: '#020604', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '900', fontFamily: 'monospace' },
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { DutyProfile } from '../../types';

interface CockpitHudScreenProps {
  duty: DutyProfile;
  speedKmh: number;
  onEndJourney: () => void;
  onBroadcastDelay: (minutes: number, reason: string) => void;
}

export function CockpitHudScreen({
  duty,
  speedKmh,
  onEndJourney,
  onBroadcastDelay,
}: CockpitHudScreenProps) {
  const [sosActive, setSosActive] = useState(false);

  const isDriveModeLocked = speedKmh > 10;

  const triggerSos = () => {
    Alert.alert(
      'EMERGENCY SOS BEACON',
      'Broadcast distress beacon to BusCon Dispatch & Police?',
      [
        { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
        {
          text: 'BROADCAST SOS',
          style: 'destructive',
          onPress: () => {
            setSosActive(true);
            Alert.alert('SOS TRANSMITTED', 'Coordinates broadcast on emergency frequency.');
          },
        },
      ]
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* Top Telemetry Line */}
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View>
          <Text style={styles.coachPlate}>{duty.busPlate}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.routeCorridor}>Kanpur Central â‡„ Lucknow ISBT</Text>
        </View>
        <View style={[styles.driveBadge, isDriveModeLocked && styles.driveBadgeLocked]}>
          <Text style={styles.driveBadgeText}>
            {isDriveModeLocked ? 'MOTION LOCKOUT' : 'IDLE / STATIONARY'}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Main Cockpit Speedometer Gauges */}
      <View style={styles.speedCircle}>
        <Text style={styles.speedValue}>{speedKmh}</Text>
        <Text style={styles.speedUnit}>KM / H</Text>
        <Text style={styles.speedLimitTag}>LIMIT: 80 KM/H</Text>
      </View>

      {/* Next Milestone Waypoint Display */}
      <View style={styles.milestoneCard}>
        <Text style={styles.milestoneSub}>APPROACHING HIGHWAY WAYPOINT</Text>
        <Text style={styles.milestoneName}>Unnao Bypass Flyover Hub</Text>
        <Text style={styles.milestoneEta}>ETA: ~14 MIN â€¢ NH-27 CLEAR</Text>
      </View>

      {/* Quick Delay Broadcast Buttons */}
      <View style={styles.actionGrid}>
        <Text style={styles.gridHeading}>BROADCAST PASSENGER DELAY</Text>
        <View style={styles.delayBtnRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.delayBtn}
            onPress={() => onBroadcastDelay(15, 'Traffic Congestion')}
            disabled={isDriveModeLocked}
          >
            <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={14} color="#F59E0B" />
            <Text style={styles.delayBtnText}>+15m Traffic</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.delayBtn}
            onPress={() => onBroadcastDelay(30, 'Highway Diversion')}
            disabled={isDriveModeLocked}
          >
            <Ionicons name="warning-outline" size={14} color="#F43F5E" />
            <Text style={styles.delayBtnText}>+30m Diversion</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Emergency & Trip End Controls */}
      <View style={styles.bottomControls}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.sosBtn} onPress={triggerSos} activeOpacity={0.8}>
          <Ionicons name="alert-circle" size={18} color="#FFFFFF" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
          <Text style={styles.sosBtnText}>{sosActive ? 'SOS TRANSMITTED' : 'EMERGENCY SOS'}</Text>

          <Text style={styles.endBtnText}>END JOURNEY</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

    phoneMasked: '+91 93â€¢â€¢â€¢ â€¢â€¢811',
    boardingStand: 'Kanpur Central Bay 4',
    dropStand: 'Lucknow Alambagh ISBT',
    status: 'PENDING',
    paymentMode: 'ONLINE',
    pnr: 'BKG-2026-77958',
  },
];

export function ManifestScreen({
  duty,
  boardedCount = 24,
  totalSeats = 40,
  manifest: initialManifest,
  onOpenSpotBooking,
}: ManifestScreenProps) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'ALL' | 'BOARDED' | 'PENDING' | 'NO_SHOW'>('ALL');
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [manifestData, setManifestData] = useState<ManifestItem[]>(
    initialManifest && initialManifest.length > 0 ? initialManifest : RANDOMIZED_MANIFEST
  );

  const safeList = Array.isArray(manifestData) ? manifestData : [];

  const filtered = safeList.filter((item) => {
    const matchesFilter = filter === 'ALL' || item.status === filter;
    const matchesSearch =
      (item.seatLabel || '').toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      (item.passengerName || '').toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      (item.pnr || '').toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      (item.boardingStand || '').toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
    return matchesFilter && matchesSearch;
  });

  const toggleStatus = (id: string) => {
    setManifestData((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) => {
        if (item.id === id) {
          const nextStatus =
            item.status === 'PENDING'
              ? 'BOARDED'
              : item.status === 'BOARDED'
              ? 'NO_SHOW'
              : 'PENDING';
          return { ...item, status: nextStatus };
        }
        return item;
      })
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* Header */}
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View>
          <Text style={styles.title}>PASSENGER ROSTER</Text>
          <Text style={styles.sub}>
            {duty?.busPlate || 'UP-78-CT-9012'} â€¢ {boardedCount}/{totalSeats} Onboard
          </Text>
        </View>

        {onOpenSpotBooking && (
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.spotAddBtn} onPress={onOpenSpotBooking} activeOpacity={0.85}>
            <Ionicons name="add" size={14} color="#020604" />
            <Text style={styles.spotAddBtnText}>SPOT TICKET</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Search Input */}
      <View style={styles.searchRow}>
        <Ionicons name="search" size={16} color={DriverTheme.colors.textMuted} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.searchInput}
          placeholder="Search Seat (A1), Name, Stand, or PNR..."
          placeholderTextColor={DriverTheme.colors.textMuted}
          value={search}
          onChangeText={setSearch}
        />
        {search.length > 0 && (
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearch('')}>
            <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={16} color={DriverTheme.colors.textMuted} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Filter Tabs */}
      <View style={styles.tabRow}>
        {(['ALL', 'BOARDED', 'PENDING', 'NO_SHOW'] as const).map((tab) => (
          <TouchableOpacity
            key={tab}
            style={[styles.tabPill, filter === tab && styles.tabPillActive]}
            onPress={() => setFilter(tab)}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.tabText, filter === tab && styles.tabTextActive]}>
              {tab.replace('_', ' ')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ))}
      </View>

      {/* Manifest Passenger Cards */}
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        {filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={styles.emptyCard}>
            <Ionicons name="people-outline" size={32} color={DriverTheme.colors.border} />
            <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>No matching passengers found</Text>
            <Text style={styles.emptySub}>Check search keywords or filter tab selection.</Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          filtered.map((pax) => {
            const isBoarded = pax.status === 'BOARDED';
            const isNoShow = pax.status === 'NO_SHOW';

            return (
              <View key={pax.id} style={styles.paxCard}>
                <View style={styles.cardTop}>
                  <View style={styles.seatBadge}>
                    <Text style={styles.seatBadgeText}>{pax.seatLabel}</Text>
                  </View>

                  <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 10 }}>
                    <Text style={styles.paxName}>{pax.passengerName}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.paxPhone}>{pax.phoneMasked} â€¢ {pax.pnr}</Text>
                  </View>

                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[
                      styles.statusPill,
                      isBoarded && styles.statusBoarded,
                      isNoShow && styles.statusNoShow,
                    ]}
                    onPress={() => toggleStatus(pax.id)}
                    activeOpacity={0.8}
                  >
                    <Ionicons
                      name={
                        isBoarded
                          ? 'checkmark-circle'
                          : isNoShow
                          ? 'close-circle'
                          : 'time-outline'
                      }
                      size={12}
                      color={isBoarded ? DriverTheme.colors.emerald : isNoShow ? DriverTheme.colors.danger : DriverTheme.colors.warning}
                      style={{ marginRight: 4 }}
                    />
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.statusText,
                        isBoarded && { color: DriverTheme.colors.emerald },
                        isNoShow && { color: DriverTheme.colors.danger },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {pax.status.replace('_', ' ')}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.routeRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.routeText}>
                    {pax.boardingStand} â†’ {pax.dropStand}
                  </Text>
                  <View style={styles.payBadge}>
                    <Text style={styles.payBadgeText}>{pax.paymentMode}</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              </View>
            );
          })
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Platform,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { DriverTheme } from '../theme/tokens';
import { DutyProfile } from '../types';

interface MapScreenProps {
  duty?: DutyProfile;
  boardedCount?: number;
  totalSeats?: number;
}

interface Waypoint {
  name: string;
  dist: string;
  passed: boolean;
  active: boolean;
}

const HIGHWAY_WAYPOINTS: Waypoint[] = [
  { name: 'Kanpur Central Bay 4 (Origin)', dist: '0.0 km', passed: true, active: false },
  { name: 'Jajmau Ganga Bridge', dist: '7.8 km', passed: true, active: false },
  { name: 'Unnao Bypass Flyover (NH-27)', dist: '24.5 km', passed: false, active: true },
  { name: 'Nawabganj Bird Sanctuary Toll', dist: '48.2 km', passed: false, active: false },
  { name: 'Bani Crossing Hub', dist: '64.0 km', passed: false, active: false },
  { name: 'Lucknow Alambagh ISBT (Terminus)', dist: '84.0 km', passed: false, active: false },
];

export function MapScreen({ duty, boardedCount = 24, totalSeats = 40 }: MapScreenProps) {
  // Live GPS Telemetry State
  const [latitude, setLatitude] = useState(26.4499);
  const [longitude, setLongitude] = useState(80.3319);
  const [speedKmh, setSpeedKmh] = useState(68);
  const [heading, setHeading] = useState(65);
  const [accuracyMeters, setAccuracyMeters] = useState(4.2);
  const [gpsStatus, setGpsStatus] = useState<'LOCK_3D' | 'SEARCHING' | 'SIMULATED'>('LOCK_3D');
  const [corridorProgress, setCorridorProgress] = useState(38); // 38% completed

  // Try real device GPS through expo-location or web navigator
  useEffect(() => {
    let watchId: any = null;

    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.geolocation) {
      watchId = navigator.geolocation.watchPosition(
        (pos) => {
          setLatitude(pos.coords.latitude);
          setLongitude(pos.coords.longitude);
          if (pos.coords.speed !== null && pos.coords.speed !== undefined) {
            setSpeedKmh(Math.round(pos.coords.speed * 3.6));
          }
          if (pos.coords.heading !== null && pos.coords.heading !== undefined) {
            setHeading(Math.round(pos.coords.heading));
          }
          if (pos.coords.accuracy) {
            setAccuracyMeters(parseFloat(pos.coords.accuracy.toFixed(1)));
          }
          setGpsStatus('LOCK_3D');
        },
        () => {
          setGpsStatus('SIMULATED');
        },
        { enableHighAccuracy: true, maximumAge: 2000, timeout: 10000 }
      );
    } else {
      // Native mobile device location polling
      (async () => {
        try {
          const Location = require('expo-location');
          const { status } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
          if (status === 'granted') {
            const sub = await Location.watchPositionAsync(
              { accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High, distanceInterval: 5 },
              (loc: any) => {
                setLatitude(loc.coords.latitude);
                setLongitude(loc.coords.longitude);
                if (loc.coords.speed && loc.coords.speed > 0) {
                  setSpeedKmh(Math.round(loc.coords.speed * 3.6));
                }
                if (loc.coords.heading) {
                  setHeading(Math.round(loc.coords.heading));
                }
                setAccuracyMeters(parseFloat((loc.coords.accuracy || 4.2).toFixed(1)));
                setGpsStatus('LOCK_3D');
              }
            );
            return () => sub.remove();
          } else {
            setGpsStatus('SIMULATED');
          }
        } catch (e) {
          setGpsStatus('SIMULATED');
        }
      })();
    }

    return () => {
      if (Platform.OS === 'web' && watchId && navigator.geolocation) {
        navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(watchId);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const handleRecenter = () => {
    Alert.alert(
      'GPS Highway Lock',
      `Locked onto AIS-140 unit.\nLat: ${latitude.toFixed(5)}\nLon: ${longitude.toFixed(5)}\nSpeed: ${speedKmh} km/h â€¢ Bearing: ${heading}Â°`
    );
  };

  const handleSos = () => {
    Alert.alert(
      'EMERGENCY SOS BROADCAST',
      `Distress signal transmitted to Central Dispatch & Highway Patrol for coach ${duty?.busPlate || 'UP-78-CT-9012'} at Lat: ${latitude.toFixed(4)}, Lon: ${longitude.toFixed(4)}.`,
      [{ text: 'DISMISS' }]
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* Top Cockpit Telemetry HUD */}
      <View style={styles.hudCard}>
        <View style={styles.hudTop}>
          <View>
            <View style={styles.statusPill}>
              <View style={[styles.pulseDot, gpsStatus === 'LOCK_3D' ? styles.pulseGreen : styles.pulseAmber]} />
              <Text style={styles.statusPillText}>
                {gpsStatus === 'LOCK_3D' ? 'AIS-140 GPS LOCKED' : 'GPS POSITION READY'}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.plateHeader}>{duty?.busPlate || 'UP-78-CT-9012'}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subText}>{duty?.busModel || 'Volvo 9600 B11R'} â€¢ NH-27 Corridor</Text>
          </View>

          {/* Big Live Speed Gauge */}
          <View style={styles.speedGauge}>
            <Text style={styles.speedVal}>{speedKmh}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.speedUnit}>KM / H</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Telemetry Sensor Bar */}
        <View style={styles.telemetryBar}>
          <View style={styles.telemetryItem}>
            <Text style={styles.telemetryLabel}>COORDS</Text>
            <Text style={styles.telemetryVal}>{latitude.toFixed(4)}Â°N, {longitude.toFixed(4)}Â°E</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.telemetryItem}>
            <Text style={styles.telemetryLabel}>BEARING</Text>
            <Text style={styles.telemetryVal}>{heading}Â° ENE</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.telemetryItem}>
            <Text style={styles.telemetryLabel}>ACCURACY</Text>
            <Text style={styles.telemetryVal}>Â±{accuracyMeters}m</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Corridor Progress Bar */}
      <View style={styles.corridorCard}>
        <View style={styles.corridorHeader}>
          <Text style={styles.corridorTitle}>CORRIDOR TRANSIT PROGRESS</Text>
          <Text style={styles.corridorPercent}>{corridorProgress}% COMPLETED</Text>
        </View>
        <View style={styles.progressBarTrack}>
          <View style={[styles.progressBarFill, { width: `${corridorProgress}%` }]} />
        </View>
        <View style={styles.corridorMeta}>
          <Text style={styles.corridorMetaText}>32.5 km Covered</Text>
          <Text style={styles.corridorMetaText}>51.5 km to Terminus (ETA ~48m)</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Visual Highway Route & Waypoints */}
      <View style={styles.routeCard}>
        <View style={styles.routeTopBar}>
          <Text style={styles.routeCardTitle}>LIVE WAYPOINTS ON NH-27</Text>
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.recenterBtn} onPress={handleRecenter}>
            <Ionicons name="locate" size={13} color="#020604" />
            <Text style={styles.recenterBtnText}>RE-CENTER</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.waypointsList}>
          {HIGHWAY_WAYPOINTS.map((wp, i) => (
            <View key={i} style={styles.waypointRow}>
              <View style={styles.waypointIconCol}>
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.waypointDot,
                    wp.passed && styles.waypointPassed,
                    wp.active && styles.waypointActive,
                  ]}
                >
                  {wp.active && <View style={styles.activeCore} />}
                </View>
                {i < HIGHWAY_WAYPOINTS.length - 1 && (
                  <View style={[styles.waypointLine, wp.passed && styles.waypointLinePassed]} />
                )}
              </View>

              <View style={styles.waypointDetails}>
                <View style={styles.waypointNameRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.waypointName, wp.active && styles.waypointNameActive]}>
                    {wp.name}
                  </Text>
                  {wp.active && (
                    <View style={styles.busMarkerPill}>
                      <Ionicons name="bus" size={10} color="#020604" />
                      <Text style={styles.busMarkerText}>COACH HERE</Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.waypointDist}>{wp.dist} from Kanpur Central</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Bottom Emergency Action Bar */}
      <View style={styles.bottomBar}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.sosBtn} onPress={handleSos} activeOpacity={0.85}>
          <Ionicons name="alert-circle" size={18} color="#FFFFFF" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
          <Text style={styles.sosBtnText}>EMERGENCY SOS DISTRESS</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  Platform,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { DriverTheme } from '../theme/tokens';
import { checkAndStoreNonce } from '../services/nonceCache';

export interface ScanRecord {
  id: string;
  pnr: string;
  seatLabel: string;
  passengerName: string;
  timestamp: string;
  status: 'VERIFIED' | 'REJECTED';
  nonce: string;
}

interface ScannerScreenProps {
  onVerifySuccess: (scanned: {
    nonce: string;
    seatLabel: string;
    pnr: string;
    passengerName: string;
  }) => void;
  recentScans?: ScanRecord[];
}

const DEFAULT_RECENT_SCANS: ScanRecord[] = [
  {
    id: 'SCN-901',
    pnr: 'BKG-2026-77810',
    seatLabel: 'A1',
    passengerName: 'Vikramaditya Rathore',
    timestamp: '13:42 IST',
    status: 'VERIFIED',
    nonce: 'NONCE-A1-8812',
  },
  {
    id: 'SCN-902',
    pnr: 'BKG-2026-77824',
    seatLabel: 'A2',
    passengerName: 'Ananya Deshmukh',
    timestamp: '13:45 IST',
    status: 'VERIFIED',
    nonce: 'NONCE-A2-9904',
  },
];

const SIMULATED_PASSES = [
  {
    pnr: 'BKG-2026-77855',
    seatLabel: 'B1',
    passengerName: 'Gurdeep Singh Ahluwalia',
    nonce: 'NONCE-B1-' + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000),
  },
  {
    pnr: 'BKG-2026-77889',
    seatLabel: 'B2',
    passengerName: 'Sneha Kulkarni',
    nonce: 'NONCE-B2-' + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000),
  },
  {
    pnr: 'BKG-2026-77912',
    seatLabel: 'C1',
    passengerName: 'Rajesh Nambiar',
    nonce: 'NONCE-C1-' + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000),
  },
];

export function ScannerScreen({
  onVerifySuccess,
  recentScans: initialScans,
}: ScannerScreenProps) {
  // Defensive state initialization guarantees recentScans is always an Array
  const [recentScans, setRecentScans] = useState<ScanRecord[]>(
    Array.isArray(initialScans) && initialScans.length > 0
      ? initialScans
      : DEFAULT_RECENT_SCANS
  );
  const [torchActive, setTorchActive] = useState(false);
  const [lastScannedSeat, setLastScannedSeat] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Safe fallback to guarantee .length is never read from undefined
  const safeRecentScans = Array.isArray(recentScans) ? recentScans : [];

  const handleSimulateScan = (pass: typeof SIMULATED_PASSES[0]) => {
    const verification = checkAndStoreNonce(pass.nonce);

    if (!verification.valid) {
      Alert.alert(
        'REJECTED',
        `PASS ALREADY BOARDED (ANTI-REPLAY VIOLATION)\nNonce: ${pass.nonce}`
      );
      return;
    }

    const newRecord: ScanRecord = {
      id: `SCN-${Date.now()}`,
      pnr: pass.pnr,
      seatLabel: pass.seatLabel,
      passengerName: pass.passengerName,
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', {
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
      }) + ' IST',
      status: 'VERIFIED',
      nonce: pass.nonce,
    };

    setRecentScans((prev) => [newRecord, ...(Array.isArray(prev) ? prev : [])]);
    setLastScannedSeat(pass.seatLabel);

    onVerifySuccess({
      nonce: pass.nonce,
      seatLabel: pass.seatLabel,
      pnr: pass.pnr,
      passengerName: pass.passengerName,
    });
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* Top HUD */}
      <View style={styles.topHud}>
        <View>
          <Text style={styles.hudTitle}>QR BOARDING SCANNER</Text>
          <Text style={styles.hudSub}>Ed25519 Tamper-Proof Cryptographic Verification</Text>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.torchBtn, torchActive && styles.torchBtnActive]}
          onPress={() => setTorchActive(!torchActive)}
        >
          <Ionicons
            name={torchActive ? 'flash' : 'flash-outline'}
            size={16}
            color={torchActive ? '#020604' : DriverTheme.colors.emerald}
          />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {/* Viewfinder Viewport */}
      <View style={styles.viewfinderCard}>
        <View style={styles.reticle}>
          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTL]} />
          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTR]} />
          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBL]} />
          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBR]} />

          <View style={styles.scanLaser} />

          <View style={styles.reticleCenter}>
            <Ionicons name="qr-code-outline" size={48} color="rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.4)" />
            <Text style={styles.reticleHint}>ALIGN PASSENGER QR CODE</Text>
            {lastScannedSeat && (
              <View style={styles.verifiedBadge}>
                <Ionicons name="checkmark-circle" size={14} color="#020604" />
                <Text style={styles.verifiedBadgeText}>SEAT {lastScannedSeat} VERIFIED</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.offlinePill}>
          OFFLINE CACHE ACTIVE • REPLAY SHIELD ENGAGED
        </Text>
      </View>

      {/* Simulator Actions for Web/Dev Testing */}
      <View style={styles.simCard}>
        <Text style={styles.simTitle}>TAP TO SIMULATE PASSENGER BOARDING SCAN:</Text>
        <View style={styles.simButtonsRow}>
          {SIMULATED_PASSES.map((pass) => (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={pass.seatLabel}
              style={styles.simBtn}
              onPress={() => handleSimulateScan(pass)}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Text style={styles.simBtnSeat}>{pass.seatLabel}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.simBtnName} numberOfLines={1}>
                {pass.passengerName.split(' ')[0]}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Recently Boarded Manifest Pings */}
      <View style={styles.logCard}>
        <Text style={styles.logHead}>RECENTLY BOARDED MANIFEST PINGS</Text>

        {safeRecentScans.length === 0 ? (
          <Text style={styles.emptyLogText}>No boarding passes scanned yet on this run.</Text>
        ) : (
          <ScrollView
            style={styles.logList}
            contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 8, paddingBottom: 20 }}
            showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          >
            {safeRecentScans.map((item) => (
              <View key={item.id} style={styles.logItem}>
                <View style={styles.logSeatBadge}>
                  <Text style={styles.logSeatBadgeText}>{item.seatLabel}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 10 }}>
                  <Text style={styles.logPassenger}>{item.passengerName}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.logMeta}>{item.pnr} • {item.timestamp}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.statusVerified}>
                  <Ionicons name="checkmark-done" size={12} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
                  <Text style={styles.statusVerifiedText}>BOARDED</Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            ))}
          </ScrollView>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: DriverTheme.colors.bg, padding: 16, gap: 12 },
  topHud: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' },

  StyleSheet,

  Text,

} from 'react-native';

interface TodayScreenProps {
  driver: DriverProfile;
  onOpenProfile: () => void;
  onStartTrip: (trip: DriverTrip) => void;
}

const INITIAL_TRIPS: DriverTrip[] = [
  {

  {
    id: 'TRP-PAST-01',
    tripReference: 'TRP-2026-00155',
    originCity: 'Kanpur',
    destinationCity: 'Lucknow',
    originTerminal: 'Kanpur Central Bay 4',
    destinationTerminal: 'Lucknow Alambagh ISBT',
    scheduledDeparture: 'Yesterday 14:00 IST',
    scheduledArrival: '15:50 IST',

    busPlate: 'UP-78-CT-9012',
    busModel: 'Volvo 9600 B11R',
    status: 'COMPLETED',
    boardedCount: 38,
    totalSeats: 40,
    distanceKm: 84,
  },
];

export function TodayScreen({ driver, onOpenProfile, onStartTrip }: TodayScreenProps) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'TODAY' | 'HISTORY'>('TODAY');
  const [trips] = useState<DriverTrip[]>(INITIAL_TRIPS);
  const [pastTrips] = useState<DriverTrip[]>(PAST_TRIPS);

  const handleStart = (trip: DriverTrip) => {
    if (driver.kycStatus !== 'VERIFIED') {
      Alert.alert('Duty Blocked', 'Driver KYC is pending admin verification.');
      return;
    }
    onStartTrip(trip);
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.avatarBtn} onPress={onOpenProfile}>
          <Text style={styles.avatarText}>{driver.name.charAt(0)}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 12 }}>
          <Text style={styles.screenTitle}>Corridor Roster</Text>
          <Text style={styles.driverSub}>{driver.name} â€¢ {driver.assignedBusPlate}</Text>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.refreshBtn} onPress={() => Alert.alert('Roster Synced', 'Schedule refreshed.')}>
          <Ionicons name="refresh" size={18} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.tabBar}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.tabItem, tab === 'TODAY' && styles.tabItemActive]} onPress={() => setTab('TODAY')}>
          <Text style={[styles.tabText, tab === 'TODAY' && styles.tabTextActive]}>TODAY ({trips.length})</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.tabItem, tab === 'HISTORY' && styles.tabItemActive]} onPress={() => setTab('HISTORY')}>
          <Text style={[styles.tabText, tab === 'HISTORY' && styles.tabTextActive]}>PAST TRIPS ({pastTrips.length})</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        {tab === 'TODAY' ? (
          trips.map((item, idx) => (
            <View key={item.id} style={styles.tripCard}>
              <View style={styles.cardTop}>
                <View>
                  <Text style={styles.routeHeader}>{item.originCity} â†’ {item.destinationCity}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.busInfo}>{item.busModel} â€¢ {item.busPlate}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.statusBadge}>
                  <Text style={styles.statusBadgeText}>{idx === 0 ? 'READY TO START' : 'UPCOMING'}</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.timeRow}>
                <View>
                  <Text style={styles.timeMain}>{item.scheduledDeparture}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.terminalSub}>{item.originTerminal}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}>
                  <Text style={styles.durationTag}>{item.durationText}</Text>
                  <Ionicons name="arrow-forward" size={14} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
                </View>
                <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
                  <Text style={styles.timeMain}>{item.scheduledArrival}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.terminalSub}>{item.destinationTerminal}</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.startBtn} onPress={() => handleStart(item)} activeOpacity={0.85}>
                <Ionicons name="navigate" size={16} color="#020604" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
                <Text style={styles.startBtnText}>START TRIP</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          ))
        ) : (
          pastTrips.map((item) => (
            <View key={item.id} style={styles.historyCard}>
              <View style={styles.cardTop}>
                <View>
                  <Text style={styles.routeHeader}>{item.originCity} â†’ {item.destinationCity}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.busInfo}>{item.scheduledDeparture}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={[styles.statusBadge, { borderColor: DriverTheme.colors.emerald }]}>
                  <Text style={[styles.statusBadgeText, { color: DriverTheme.colors.emerald }]}>COMPLETED</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

                <Text style={styles.historyStatItem}>Distance: {item.distanceKm} km</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          ))
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { DriverTheme } from '../theme/tokens';
import { DriverTrip, DelayReason } from '../types';
import { DelayModal } from '../components/DelayModal';

interface TripScreenProps {
  trip: DriverTrip;
  onNavigateScan: () => void;
  onNavigateManifest: () => void;
  onNavigateMap: () => void;
  onEndTrip: () => void;
  onBackToToday: () => void;
}

export function TripScreen({
  trip,
  onNavigateScan,
  onNavigateManifest,
  onNavigateMap,
  onEndTrip,
  onBackToToday,
}: TripScreenProps) {
  const [delayModalVisible, setDelayModalVisible] = useState(false);
  const [reportedDelay, setReportedDelay] = useState<{ min: number; reason: DelayReason } | null>(null);

  const handleEndAttempt = () => {
    Alert.alert(
      'End Active Trip?',
      'You have pending unboarded seats. Are you sure you want to end this trip and go off-duty?',
      [
        { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
        { text: 'Confirm End Trip', style: 'destructive', onPress: onEndTrip },
      ]
    );
  };

  const handleBroadcastDelay = (min: number, reason: DelayReason) => {
    setReportedDelay({ min, reason });
    Alert.alert('Delay Broadcasted', `+${min}m delay broadcast to passengers (${reason}).`);
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.backBtn}
          onPress={() => {
            Alert.alert('Trip In Progress', 'Trip and GPS will continue running in background. Return to today?', [
              { text: 'Stay', style: 'cancel' },
              { text: 'Back to Today', onPress: onBackToToday },
            ]);
          }}
        >
          <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={18} color={DriverTheme.colors.textBright} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <Text style={styles.refText}>{trip.tripReference}</Text>
        <Ionicons name="bus" size={18} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
      </View>

      <View style={styles.summaryCard}>
        <View style={styles.cardHeader}>
          <Text style={styles.routeText}>{trip.originCity} â†’ {trip.destinationCity}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.plateText}>{trip.busPlate}</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.terminalText}>{trip.originTerminal} â†’ {trip.destinationTerminal}</Text>
        <View style={styles.timesRow}>
          <Text style={styles.timeTag}>Dep: {trip.scheduledDeparture}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.timeTag}>Arr: {trip.scheduledArrival}</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.statusRow}>
        <View style={styles.dutyPill}>
          <View style={styles.greenDot} />
          <Text style={styles.dutyPillText}>ON DUTY</Text>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.gpsPill} onPress={onNavigateMap}>
          <Ionicons name="radio" size={12} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
          <Text style={styles.gpsPillText}>GPS STREAM ACTIVE (VIEW MAP)</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {reportedDelay && (
        <View style={styles.delayBanner}>
          <Ionicons name="alert-circle" size={16} color={DriverTheme.colors.warning} />
          <Text style={styles.delayBannerText}>
            Reported: +{reportedDelay.min}m delay ({reportedDelay.reason})
          </Text>
        </View>
      )}

      <View style={styles.actionsGrid}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.actionCard} onPress={onNavigateScan}>
          <View style={styles.iconCircle}>
            <Ionicons name="qr-code" size={28} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.actionTitle}>SCAN QR PASS</Text>
          <Text style={styles.actionSub}>Verify passenger boarding passes</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.actionCard} onPress={onNavigateManifest}>
          <View style={styles.iconCircle}>
            <Ionicons name="list" size={28} color={DriverTheme.colors.emerald} />
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.actionSub}>Passenger roster & no-show control</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <TouchableOpacity style={styles.delayTriggerBtn} onPress={() => setDelayModalVisible(true)}>
        <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={16} color={DriverTheme.colors.warning} />
        <Text style={styles.delayTriggerText}>REPORT CORRIDOR DELAY</Text>

        <Text style={styles.endBtnText}>END TRIP & FINALIZE CORRIDOR</Text>

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { useCleanTheme } from '../../theme/clean';
import { apiCall, API_BASE } from '../../lib/api/clean';
import { getDeviceFingerprint } from '../../lib/auth/fingerprint';
import { saveSession, StoredSession } from '../../lib/auth/storage';

interface Props {
  phone: string;
  onBack: () => void;
  onVerified: (session: StoredSession) => void;
}

interface VerifyResponse {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  expiresAt: string;
  user: { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string };
}

export function Otp({ phone, onBack, onVerified }: Props) {
  const t = useCleanTheme();
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errCode, setErrCode] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const submit = async () => {
    setError(null);
    setErrCode(null);
    if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(otp)) {
      setError('Enter the 6-digit code');
      return;
    }
    console.log('[OTP] submitting');
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const fingerprint = await getDeviceFingerprint();
      console.log('[OTP] fingerprint length:', fingerprint.length);
      const res = await apiCall<VerifyResponse>('/driver/auth/otp/verify', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { phone, otp, deviceFingerprint: fingerprint },
      });
      console.log('[OTP] verify success for', res.user.phone);
      const session: StoredSession = {
        token: res.accessToken,
        user: { id: res.user.id, name: res.user.name, phone: res.user.phone },
      };
      await saveSession(session);
      onVerified(session);
    } catch (e: any) {
      const code = e?.code ?? 'UNKNOWN';
      const msg = e?.message ?? 'Could not verify OTP';
      console.error('[OTP] failed:', code, msg);
      setErrCode(code);
      setError(msg);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.root} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
      <View style={styles.top}>
        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: t.colors.text }]}>Enter the code</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.subtitle, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
          We sent a 6-digit code to {phone}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.form}>
        <TextInput
          style={[styles.input, {
            borderColor: t.colors.border,
            backgroundColor: t.colors.surface,
            color: t.colors.text,
          }]}
          value={otp}
          onChangeText={setOtp}
          keyboardType="number-pad"
          maxLength={6}
          placeholder="123456"
          placeholderTextColor={t.colors.textMuted}
          editable={!loading}
        />

        {error ? (
          <View style={[styles.errorBox, { backgroundColor: t.colors.dangerSoft, borderColor: t.colors.danger }]}>
            <Text style={[styles.errorText, { color: t.colors.danger }]}>{error}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.errorMeta, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>{errCode} - {API_BASE}</Text>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        <Pressable
          onPress={submit}
          disabled={loading}
          style={({ pressed }) => [styles.button, {
            backgroundColor: t.colors.primary,
            opacity: loading ? 0.6 : pressed ? 0.9 : 1,
          }]}
        >
          {loading
            ? <ActivityIndicator color={t.colors.primaryText} />
            : <Text style={[styles.buttonText, { color: t.colors.primaryText }]}>Verify</Text>}
        </Pressable>

        <Pressable onPress={onBack} style={styles.backBtn}>
          <Text style={[styles.backText, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>Change phone</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { padding: 24, gap: 32, flexGrow: 1, justifyContent: 'center' },
  top: { alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 },
  title: { fontSize: 24, fontWeight: '800', textAlign: 'center' },
  subtitle: { fontSize: 14, textAlign: 'center', maxWidth: 340 },
  form: { gap: 8 },
  input: { borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 12, paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingVertical: 16, fontSize: 24, letterSpacing: 12, textAlign: 'center' },
  errorBox: { marginTop: 8, padding: 12, borderRadius: 10, borderWidth: 1, gap: 4 },
  errorText: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '600' },
  errorMeta: { fontSize: 11 },
  button: { marginTop: 16, borderRadius: 12, paddingVertical: 16, alignItems: 'center' },
  buttonText: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800' },
  backBtn: { paddingVertical: 12, alignItems: 'center' },
  backText: { fontSize: 13 },
});

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, TextInput, Pressable, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { useCleanTheme } from '../../theme/clean';
import { apiCall, API_BASE } from '../../lib/api/clean';

interface Props {
  onSent: (phone: string) => void;
}

export function Phone({ onSent }: Props) {
  const t = useCleanTheme();
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('+919876540011');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errCode, setErrCode] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const submit = async () => {
    setError(null);
    setErrCode(null);
    const cleaned = phone.trim();
    if (!/^\+91\d{10}$/.test(cleaned)) {
      setError('Enter a valid +91 phone number (example: +919876540011)');
      return;
    }
    console.log('[PHONE] submitting for', cleaned);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await apiCall<{ expiresInSeconds: number }>('/driver/auth/otp/request', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { phone: cleaned },
      });
      console.log('[PHONE] OTP sent');
      onSent(cleaned);
    } catch (e: any) {
      const code = e?.code ?? 'UNKNOWN';
      const msg = e?.message ?? 'Could not send OTP';
      console.error('[PHONE] failed:', code, msg);
      setErrCode(code);
      setError(msg);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.root} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
      <View style={styles.top}>
        <View style={[styles.mark, { backgroundColor: t.colors.primary }]}>
          <Text style={styles.markLetter}>B</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: t.colors.text }]}>BusVerse Driver</Text>
        <Text style={[styles.subtitle, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
          Sign in with the phone number your operator invited you on
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.form}>
        <Text style={[styles.label, { color: t.colors.textSecondary }]}>Phone number</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={[styles.input, {
            borderColor: t.colors.border,
            backgroundColor: t.colors.surface,
            color: t.colors.text,
          }]}
          value={phone}
          onChangeText={setPhone}
          placeholder="+919876543210"
          placeholderTextColor={t.colors.textMuted}
          keyboardType="phone-pad"
          autoCapitalize="none"
          editable={!loading}
        />

        {error ? (
          <View style={[styles.errorBox, { backgroundColor: t.colors.dangerSoft, borderColor: t.colors.danger }]}>
            <Text style={[styles.errorText, { color: t.colors.danger }]}>{error}</Text>
            <Text style={[styles.errorMeta, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>{errCode} - {API_BASE}</Text>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        <Pressable
          onPress={submit}
          disabled={loading}
          style={({ pressed }) => [styles.button, {
            backgroundColor: t.colors.primary,
            opacity: loading ? 0.6 : pressed ? 0.9 : 1,
          }]}
        >
          {loading
            ? <ActivityIndicator color={t.colors.primaryText} />
            : <Text style={[styles.buttonText, { color: t.colors.primaryText }]}>Continue</Text>}
        </Pressable>

        <Text style={[styles.hint, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>Dev mode: OTP is 123456</Text>
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
}
