import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, FlatList, StyleSheet, ActivityIndicator, Pressable, RefreshControl } from 'react-native';
import { useCleanTheme } from '../../theme/clean';
import { apiCall, API_BASE } from '../../lib/api/clean';
import { StoredSession } from '../../lib/auth/storage';

interface TripSummary {
  tripId: string;
  scheduleId: string;
  bus: { id: string; registrationNumber: string; model: string | null };
  route: { originCity: string; destinationCity: string };
  departureTime: string;
  arrivalTime: string;
  status: 'SCHEDULED' | 'IN_PROGRESS' | 'COMPLETED' | 'CANCELLED';
  boardedCount: number;
  confirmedCount: number;
}

interface Props {
  session: StoredSession;
  onLogout: () => void;
}

function fmtTime(iso: string): string {
  try {
    return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', {
      timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      hour12: false,
    }) + ' IST';
  } catch {
    return iso;
  }
}

export function Trips({ session, onLogout }: Props) {
  const t = useCleanTheme();
  const [trips, setTrips] = useState<TripSummary[] | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = async (isRefresh = false) => {
    if (isRefresh) setRefreshing(true);
    else setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const rows = await apiCall<TripSummary[]>('/driver/trips', { token: session.token });
      console.log('[TRIPS] loaded', rows.length, 'trip(s)');
      setTrips(rows);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('[TRIPS] failed:', e?.code, e?.message);
      setError(e?.message ?? 'Could not load trips');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => { void load(); }, []);

  const renderTrip = ({ item }: { item: TripSummary }) => {
    const tone =
      item.status === 'IN_PROGRESS' ? { bg: t.colors.successSoft, fg: t.colors.success } :
      item.status === 'COMPLETED'   ? { bg: t.colors.surfaceElevated, fg: t.colors.textMuted } :
      item.status === 'CANCELLED'   ? { bg: t.colors.dangerSoft, fg: t.colors.danger } :
      { bg: t.colors.primarySoft, fg: t.colors.primary };

    return (
      <View style={[styles.card, { backgroundColor: t.colors.surface, borderColor: t.colors.border }]}>
        <View style={styles.cardTop}>
          <Text style={[styles.route, { color: t.colors.text }]}>
            {item.route.originCity} to {item.route.destinationCity}
          </Text>
          <View style={[styles.badge, { backgroundColor: tone.bg }]}>
            <Text style={[styles.badgeText, { color: tone.fg }]}>{item.status.replace('_', ' ')}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        <Text style={[styles.detail, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
          {item.bus.model ?? 'Bus'} {item.bus.registrationNumber}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.detail, { color: t.colors.textSecondary }]}>
          {fmtTime(item.departureTime)} to {fmtTime(item.arrivalTime)}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.detail, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
          Boarded {item.boardedCount} of {item.confirmedCount}
        </Text>
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: t.colors.bg }]}>
      <View style={[styles.header, { borderBottomColor: t.colors.border }]}>
        <View>
          <Text style={[styles.hi, { color: t.colors.text }]}>Hi, {session.user.name ?? 'Driver'}</Text>
          <Text style={[styles.subtle, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>Today's trips</Text>
        </View>
        <Pressable onPress={onLogout} style={[styles.logoutBtn, { borderColor: t.colors.border }]}>
          <Text style={[styles.logoutText, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>Log out</Text>
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      {loading ? (
        <View style={styles.center}><ActivityIndicator size="large" color={t.colors.primary} /></View>
      ) : error ? (
        <View style={styles.center}>
          <Text style={[styles.errorText, { color: t.colors.danger }]}>{error}</Text>
          <Text style={[styles.errorMeta, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>{API_BASE}</Text>
          <Pressable onPress={() => load()} style={[styles.retryBtn, { backgroundColor: t.colors.primary }]}>
            <Text style={{ color: t.colors.primaryText, fontWeight: '700' }}>Retry</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      ) : !trips || trips.length === 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.center}>
          <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: t.colors.text }]}>No trips today</Text>
          <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: t.colors.textMuted }]}>
            Your operator hasn't assigned any trips for today.
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          data={trips}
          keyExtractor={(it) => it.tripId}
          renderItem={renderTrip}
          contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, gap: 12 }}
          refreshControl={
            <RefreshControl
              refreshing={refreshing}
              onRefresh={() => load(true)}
              tintColor={t.colors.primary}
            />
          }
        />
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1 },
  header: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', paddingHorizontal: 20, paddingTop: 20, paddingBottom: 16, borderBottomWidth: 1 },
  hi: { fontSize: 20, fontWeight: '800' },
  subtle: { fontSize: 13, marginTop: 2 },
  logoutBtn: { paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 8, borderRadius: 8, borderWidth: 1 },
  logoutText: { fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '600' },
  card: { padding: 16, borderRadius: 14, borderWidth: 1, gap: 6 },
  cardTop: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' },
  route: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800' },
  badge: { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 8 },
  badgeText: { fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '700' },
  detail: { fontSize: 13 },
  center: { flex: 1, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: 32, gap: 12 },
  errorText: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: '700', textAlign: 'center' },
  errorMeta: { fontSize: 11, textAlign: 'center' },
  emptyTitle: { fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '800' },

});

const scannedNonces = new Set<string>();

export function checkAndStoreNonce(nonce: string): { valid: boolean; reason?: string } {
  if (!nonce) return { valid: false, reason: 'MISSING_NONCE' };
  if (scannedNonces.has(nonce)) return { valid: false, reason: 'REPLAYED_PASS_ALREADY_BOARDED' };
  scannedNonces.add(nonce);
  return { valid: true };
}

FILE: apps\api\src\modules\trip\trip.router.ts

import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { requireRole } from '../../middleware/require-role.js';
import * as c from './trip.controller.js';

function isDomainError(
  err: unknown,
): err is { code: string; httpStatus: number; message: string; details?: Record<string, unknown> } {
  if (!err || typeof err !== 'object') return false;
  const e = err as Record<string, unknown>;
  return typeof e.code === 'string' && typeof e.httpStatus === 'number' && typeof e.message === 'string';
}

function isZodError(err: unknown): err is { issues: unknown[] } {
  if (!err || typeof err !== 'object') return false;
  const e = err as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (!Array.isArray(e.issues)) return false;
  const ctor = (err as { constructor?: { name?: string } }).constructor;
  return (err as { name?: string }).name === 'ZodError' || ctor?.name === 'ZodError';
}

export async function tripRouter(server: FastifyInstance): Promise<void> {
  server.setErrorHandler((error, request, reply) => {
    if (isDomainError(error)) {
      return reply.status(error.httpStatus).send({
        error: {
          code: error.code,
          message: error.message,
          ...(error.details ? { details: error.details } : {}),
          requestId: request.id,
        },
      });
    }
    if (isZodError(error)) {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        error: {
          code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
          message: 'Validation failed',
          details: { issues: error.issues },
          requestId: request.id,
        },
      });
    }
    const anyErr = error as { statusCode?: number; code?: string; message?: string };
    const statusCode = anyErr.statusCode ?? 500;
    const isProd = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
    return reply.status(statusCode).send({
      error: {
        code: anyErr.code ?? 'INTERNAL_ERROR',
        message: isProd ? 'Something went wrong' : (anyErr.message ?? 'Unknown error'),
        requestId: request.id,
      },
    });
  });

  const auth = { preHandler: requireRole('DRIVER') };

  server.get('/driver/config', auth, c.getConfig);
  server.get('/driver/me', auth, c.getMe);
  server.get('/driver/trips', auth, c.getMyTrips);
  server.get('/driver/trips/history', auth, c.getHistory);
  server.get('/driver/trips/:id', auth, c.getTrip);
  server.post('/driver/trips/:id/start', auth, c.postStartTrip);
  server.post('/driver/trips/:id/end', auth, c.postEndTrip);
  server.get('/driver/trips/:id/manifest', auth, c.getManifest);
  server.post('/driver/trips/:id/gps', auth, c.postGps);
  server.post('/driver/trips/:id/scan', auth, c.postScan);
  server.post('/driver/trips/:id/delay', auth, c.postDelay);
  server.post('/driver/trips/:id/cancel', auth, c.postCancelTrip);
  server.post('/driver/uploads', auth, c.postDriverUploads);
  server.post('/driver/me/documents', auth, c.postDriverDocuments);
}

import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  DELAY_REASON,
  DELAY_MIN_MINUTES,
  DELAY_MAX_MINUTES,
  DELAY_MAX_NOTES_LENGTH,
} from './trip.types.js';

export const tripIdParamSchema = z.object({ id: z.string().uuid() });

export const gpsBodySchema = z
  .object({
    lat: z.number().min(-90).max(90),
    lng: z.number().min(-180).max(180),
    speedKmh: z.number().min(0).max(300),
    accuracyM: z.number().min(0).max(1000),
    recordedAt: z.string().datetime(),
  })
  .strict();

export const delayBodySchema = z
  .object({
    reason: z.enum(DELAY_REASON),
    delayMinutes: z.number().int().min(DELAY_MIN_MINUTES).max(DELAY_MAX_MINUTES),
    notes: z.string().max(DELAY_MAX_NOTES_LENGTH).optional(),
  })
  .strict();

export const tripListQuerySchema = z.object({
  date: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, 'Expected YYYY-MM-DD')
    .optional(),
});

export const historyQuerySchema = z.object({
  from: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).optional(),
  to: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).optional(),
  limit: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(100).default(20),
  cursor: z.string().optional(),
});

export const scanBodySchema = z
  .object({
    qrPayload: z.string().min(1).max(800).optional(),
    scannedAt: z.string().datetime(),
    scannedLat: z.number().min(-90).max(90).optional(),
    scannedLng: z.number().min(-180).max(180).optional(),
    manualEntry: z.boolean().optional(),
    bookingReference: z.string().min(1).max(30).optional(),
    primaryPassengerName: z.string().min(1).max(100).optional(),
  })
  .strict()
  .refine(
    (d) => {
      if (d.manualEntry) return !!d.bookingReference && !!d.primaryPassengerName;
      return !!d.qrPayload;
    },
    { message: 'Provide qrPayload, OR manualEntry + bookingReference + primaryPassengerName' },
  );
export const uploadUrlBodySchema = z
  .object({
    filename: z.string().min(1).max(200),
    contentType: z.enum(['application/pdf', 'image/jpeg', 'image/png']),
    purpose: z.enum(['DRIVER_LICENSE', 'GOVERNMENT_ID', 'PROFILE_PHOTO']),
  })
  .strict();

export const attachDocsBodySchema = z
  .object({
    uploadIds: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(80)).min(1).max(3),
  })
  .strict();
export const cancelTripBodySchema = z
  .object({
    reason: z.string().min(5).max(500),
  })
  .strict();

import type { FastifyReply, FastifyRequest } from 'fastify';
import { success } from '../../lib/response.js';
import * as schema from './trip.schema.js';
import * as svc from './trip.service.js';
import * as gps from './gps.service.js';
import * as scan from './scan.service.js';

function actorIp(request: FastifyRequest): string | undefined {
  const xff = request.headers['x-forwarded-for'];
  if (typeof xff === 'string') return xff.split(',')[0]?.trim();
  return request.ip;
}

// ---------- Driver trip surface ----------

export async function getMyTrips(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const q = schema.tripListQuerySchema.parse(request.query ?? {});
  const rows = await svc.listDriverTrips(request.user!.userId, { date: q.date });
  return reply.status(200).send(success(rows));
}

export async function getTrip(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const { id } = schema.tripIdParamSchema.parse(request.params);
  const row = await svc.getTripDetail(request.user!.userId, id);
  return reply.status(200).send(success(row));
}

export async function postStartTrip(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const { id } = schema.tripIdParamSchema.parse(request.params);
  const row = await svc.startTrip(request.user!.userId, id, actorIp(request));
  return reply.status(200).send(success(row));
}

export async function postEndTrip(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const { id } = schema.tripIdParamSchema.parse(request.params);
  const row = await svc.endTrip(request.user!.userId, id, actorIp(request));
  return reply.status(200).send(success(row));
}

export async function getManifest(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const { id } = schema.tripIdParamSchema.parse(request.params);
  const row = await svc.getManifest(request.user!.userId, id);
  return reply.status(200).send(success(row));
}

export async function getHistory(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const q = schema.historyQuerySchema.parse(request.query ?? {});
  const result = await svc.listHistory(request.user!.userId, {
    from: q.from,
    to: q.to,
    limit: q.limit,
    cursor: q.cursor,
  });
  return reply.status(200).send(success(result.data, { nextCursor: result.nextCursor }));
}
// ---------- GPS + scan ----------

export async function postGps(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const { id } = schema.tripIdParamSchema.parse(request.params);
  const body = schema.gpsBodySchema.parse(request.body ?? {});
  const session = await import('../../lib/prisma.js').then((m) =>
    m.prisma.session.findUnique({
      where: { id: request.user!.sessionId },
      select: { deviceFingerprintHash: true },
    }),
  );
  const result = await gps.ingestGpsSample(
    request.user!.userId,
    id,
    {
      lat: body.lat,
      lng: body.lng,
      speedKmh: body.speedKmh,
      accuracyM: body.accuracyM,
      recordedAt: body.recordedAt,
    },
    {
      sessionDeviceHash: session?.deviceFingerprintHash ?? null,
      activeSessionDeviceHash: session?.deviceFingerprintHash ?? null,
    },
  );
  return reply.status(200).send(success(result));
}

export async function postScan(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const { id } = schema.tripIdParamSchema.parse(request.params);
  const body = schema.scanBodySchema.parse(request.body ?? {});
  const result = await scan.scanTicket(request.user!.userId, id, body, actorIp(request));
  return reply.status(200).send(success(result));
}

// ---------- Delay ----------

export async function postDelay(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const { id } = schema.tripIdParamSchema.parse(request.params);
  const body = schema.delayBodySchema.parse(request.body ?? {});
  const result = await svc.reportDelay(
    request.user!.userId,
    id,
    { reason: body.reason, delayMinutes: body.delayMinutes, notes: body.notes },
    actorIp(request),
  );
  return reply.status(200).send(success(result));
}

// ---------- Me + config ----------

export async function getMe(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const row = await svc.getDriverMe(request.user!.userId);
  return reply.status(200).send(success(row));
}

export async function getConfig(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const row = await svc.getDriverConfig(request.user!.userId);
  return reply.status(200).send(success(row));
}
// ---------- Uploads (buscon/02 Â§14, mock) ----------

export async function postDriverUploads(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = schema.uploadUrlBodySchema.parse(request.body ?? {});
  const result = await svc.createDriverUploadUrl(request.user!.userId, body);
  return reply.status(201).send(success(result));
}

export async function postDriverDocuments(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = schema.attachDocsBodySchema.parse(request.body ?? {});
  const result = await svc.attachDriverDocuments(request.user!.userId, body, actorIp(request));
  return reply.status(200).send(success(result));
}

export async function postCancelTrip(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const { id } = schema.tripIdParamSchema.parse(request.params);
  const body = schema.cancelTripBodySchema.parse(request.body ?? {});
  const result = await svc.cancelTrip(request.user!.userId, id, { reason: body.reason }, actorIp(request));
  return reply.status(200).send(success(result));
}

import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import * as c from './identity.controller.js';
import { requireRole } from '../../middleware/require-role.js';
import { getPassengerMe } from './passenger.profile.controller.js';

/**
 * Plugin-scoped error handler. Guarantees DomainErrors map to the right
 * HTTP status for identity routes, regardless of ordering issues in the
 * parent app's `setErrorHandler` registration.
 */
function isDomainError(
  err: unknown,
): err is { code: string; httpStatus: number; message: string; details?: Record<string, unknown> } {
  if (!err || typeof err !== 'object') return false;
  const e = err as Record<string, unknown>;
  return (
    typeof e.code === 'string' &&
    typeof e.httpStatus === 'number' &&
    typeof e.message === 'string'
  );
}

function isZodError(err: unknown): err is { issues: unknown[]; message: string } {
  if (!err || typeof err !== 'object') return false;
  const e = err as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (!Array.isArray(e.issues)) return false;
  const ctor = (err as { constructor?: { name?: string } }).constructor;
  return e.name === 'ZodError' || ctor?.name === 'ZodError';
}

export async function identityRouter(server: FastifyInstance): Promise<void> {
  // Scoped error handler â€” takes precedence for routes in this plugin
  server.setErrorHandler((error, request, reply) => {
    if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'test') {
      console.log('[err-handler-ROUTER]', {
        url: request.url,
        errName: (error as { name?: string })?.name,
        errCtor: (error as { constructor?: { name?: string } })?.constructor?.name,
        code: (error as { code?: unknown })?.code,
        httpStatus: (error as { httpStatus?: unknown })?.httpStatus,
        hasIssues: Array.isArray((error as { issues?: unknown })?.issues),
        isDomain: isDomainError(error),
        isZod: isZodError(error),
      });
    }

    if (isDomainError(error)) {
      return reply.status(error.httpStatus).send({
        error: {
          code: error.code,
          message: error.message,
          ...(error.details ? { details: error.details } : {}),
          requestId: request.id,
        },
      });
    }

    if (isZodError(error)) {
      return reply.status(422).send({
        error: {
          code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
          message: 'Validation failed',
          details: { issues: error.issues },
          requestId: request.id,
        },
      });
    }

    const anyErr = error as { statusCode?: number; code?: string; message?: string };
    const statusCode = anyErr.statusCode ?? 500;
    const isProd = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
    return reply.status(statusCode).send({
      error: {
        code: anyErr.code ?? 'INTERNAL_ERROR',
        message: isProd ? 'Something went wrong' : (anyErr.message ?? 'Unknown error'),
        requestId: request.id,
      },
    });
  });

  // â”€â”€ Routes â”€â”€
  server.post('/passenger/auth/otp/request', c.postPassengerOtpRequest);
  server.post('/passenger/auth/otp/verify', c.postPassengerOtpVerify);
  server.get('/passenger/me', { preHandler: requireRole('REGISTERED_PASSENGER') }, getPassengerMe);

  server.post('/driver/auth/otp/request', c.postDriverOtpRequest);
  server.post('/driver/auth/otp/verify', c.postDriverOtpVerify);

  server.post('/operator/auth/signup', c.postOperatorSignup);
  server.post('/operator/auth/login', c.postOperatorLogin);
  server.post('/operator/auth/otp/request', c.postOperatorOtpRequest);
  server.post('/operator/auth/otp/verify', c.postOperatorOtpVerify);
  server.post('/operator/auth/reset/request', c.postOperatorResetRequest);
  server.post('/operator/auth/reset/verify', c.postOperatorResetVerify);

  server.post('/admin/auth/login', c.postAdminLogin);
  server.post('/admin/auth/reset/request', c.postAdminResetRequest);
  server.post('/admin/auth/reset/verify', c.postAdminResetVerify);

  server.post('/:surface/auth/refresh', c.postRefresh);
  server.post('/:surface/auth/logout', c.postLogout);
}

import { z } from 'zod';

const phoneSchema = z
  .string()
  .min(10)
  .max(20)
  .regex(/^\+?[1-9]\d{1,14}$/, 'Invalid phone format');

const emailSchema = z.string().email().max(255);
const otpSchema = z.string().regex(/^\d{6}$/, 'OTP must be 6 digits');

const operatorPasswordSchema = z.string().min(12).max(128);
const adminPasswordSchema = z.string().min(16).max(128);

export const passengerOtpRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    phone: phoneSchema.optional(),
    email: emailSchema.optional(),
    intent: z.enum(['LOGIN', 'SIGNUP']),
  })
  .refine(
    (v) => (v.phone && !v.email) || (!v.phone && v.email),
    'Provide exactly one of phone or email',
  );

export const passengerOtpVerifySchema = z
  .object({
    phone: phoneSchema.optional(),
    email: emailSchema.optional(),
    otp: otpSchema,
  })
  .refine(
    (v) => (v.phone && !v.email) || (!v.phone && v.email),
    'Provide exactly one of phone or email',
  );

export const driverOtpRequestSchema = z.object({ phone: phoneSchema });

export const driverOtpVerifySchema = z.object({
  phone: phoneSchema,
  otp: otpSchema,
  deviceFingerprint: z.string().min(8).max(256),
});

export const operatorOtpRequestSchema = z.object({ phone: phoneSchema });

export const operatorOtpVerifySchema = z.object({
  phone: phoneSchema,
  otp: otpSchema,
});

export const operatorLoginSchema = z.object({
  email: emailSchema,
  password: z.string().min(1).max(128),
});

export const adminLoginSchema = z.object({
  email: emailSchema,
  password: z.string().min(1).max(128),
});

export const passwordResetRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    email: emailSchema.optional(),
    phone: phoneSchema.optional(),
  })
  .refine(
    (v) => (v.email && !v.phone) || (!v.email && v.phone),
    'Provide exactly one of email or phone',
  );

const resetVerifyBase = z
  .object({
    token: z.string().min(20).max(200).optional(),
    phone: phoneSchema.optional(),
    otp: otpSchema.optional(),
  })
  .refine((v) => {
    const hasToken = !!v.token;
    const hasOtp = !!(v.phone && v.otp);
    return hasToken !== hasOtp;
  }, 'Provide either token (email path) or phone+otp (fallback path), not both');

export const operatorPasswordResetVerifySchema = resetVerifyBase.and(
  z.object({ newPassword: operatorPasswordSchema }),
);

export const adminPasswordResetVerifySchema = resetVerifyBase.and(
  z.object({ newPassword: adminPasswordSchema }),
);

export const refreshSchema = z.object({});
export const logoutSchema = z.object({});

export const surfaceParamSchema = z.object({
  surface: z.enum(['passenger', 'driver', 'operator', 'admin']),
});
const gstinSchema = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^[0-9]{2}[A-Z]{5}[0-9]{4}[A-Z][1-9A-Z]Z[0-9A-Z]$/, 'Invalid GSTIN format');

const panSchema = z
  .string()
  .regex(/^[A-Z]{5}[0-9]{4}[A-Z]$/, 'Invalid PAN format');

export const operatorSignupSchema = z
  .object({
    companyName: z.string().min(2).max(200),
    gstin: gstinSchema,
    pan: panSchema,
    email: emailSchema,
    phone: phoneSchema,
    password: operatorPasswordSchema,
  })
  .strict();

import type { FastifyReply, FastifyRequest } from 'fastify';
import { success } from '../../lib/response.js';
import { errors } from '../../lib/errors.js';
import * as service from './identity.service.js';
import * as schema from './identity.schema.js';
import {
  passengerOtpRequestSchema,
  passengerOtpVerifySchema,
  driverOtpRequestSchema,
  driverOtpVerifySchema,
  operatorOtpRequestSchema,
  operatorOtpVerifySchema,
  operatorLoginSchema,
  adminLoginSchema,
  passwordResetRequestSchema,
  operatorPasswordResetVerifySchema,
  adminPasswordResetVerifySchema,
  refreshSchema,
  surfaceParamSchema,
} from './identity.schema.js';
import { redis, keys } from '../../lib/redis.js';
import { verifyAccessToken } from './jwt.service.js';

function ctx(req: FastifyRequest) {
  return {
    ip: req.ip,
    userAgent: req.headers['user-agent'] ?? undefined,
  };
}

function extractRefreshToken(req: FastifyRequest): string {
  const body = req.body as { refreshToken?: string } | undefined;
  if (body && typeof body.refreshToken === 'string') return body.refreshToken;

  const header = req.headers.authorization;
  if (header && header.startsWith('Bearer ')) return header.slice(7).trim();

  throw errors.unauthorized('Missing refresh token');
}

export async function postPassengerOtpRequest(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = passengerOtpRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.passengerOtpRequest({ ...body, ip: req.ip });
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postPassengerOtpVerify(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = passengerOtpVerifySchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.passengerOtpVerify({ ...body, ...ctx(req) });
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postDriverOtpRequest(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = driverOtpRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.driverOtpRequest({ ...body, ip: req.ip });
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postDriverOtpVerify(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = driverOtpVerifySchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.driverOtpVerify({ ...body, ...ctx(req) });
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postOperatorOtpRequest(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = operatorOtpRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.operatorOtpRequest({ ...body, ip: req.ip });
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postOperatorOtpVerify(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = operatorOtpVerifySchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.operatorOtpVerify({ ...body, ...ctx(req) });
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postOperatorLogin(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = operatorLoginSchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.operatorLogin({ ...body, ...ctx(req) });
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postAdminLogin(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = adminLoginSchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.adminLogin({ ...body, ...ctx(req) });
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postOperatorResetRequest(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = passwordResetRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.passwordResetRequest(body, 'operator');
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postOperatorResetVerify(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = operatorPasswordResetVerifySchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.passwordResetVerifyOperator(body);
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postAdminResetRequest(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = passwordResetRequestSchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.passwordResetRequest(body, 'admin');
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postAdminResetVerify(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = adminPasswordResetVerifySchema.parse(req.body);
  const result = await service.passwordResetVerifyAdmin(body);
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postRefresh(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  surfaceParamSchema.parse(req.params);
  refreshSchema.parse(req.body ?? {});
  const refreshToken = extractRefreshToken(req);
  const result = await service.refreshSession(refreshToken);
  return reply.send(success(result));
}

export async function postLogout(req: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  surfaceParamSchema.parse(req.params);
  if (!req.user) throw errors.unauthorized();

  const header = req.headers.authorization;
  let ttlSeconds = 300;
  if (header && header.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    try {
      const claims = verifyAccessToken(header.slice(7));
      ttlSeconds = Math.max(1, claims.exp - Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  }

  await service.logout(req.user.sessionId, req.user.userId);
  await redis.set(keys.revokedSession(req.user.sessionId), '1', 'EX', ttlSeconds);

  return reply.status(204).send();
}
export async function postOperatorSignup(request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) {
  const body = schema.operatorSignupSchema.parse(request.body ?? {});
  const xff = request.headers['x-forwarded-for'];
  const ip = typeof xff === 'string' ? xff.split(',')[0]?.trim() : request.ip;
  const result = await service.operatorSignup({
    companyName: body.companyName,
    gstin: body.gstin,
    pan: body.pan,
    email: body.email,
    phone: body.phone,
    password: body.password,
    ip,
  });
  return reply.status(201).send(success(result));
}

model Schedule {

model Booking {
  id                  String    @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  bookingReference    String    @map("booking_reference") @db.VarChar(20)
  userId              String    @map("user_id") @db.Uuid
  scheduleId          String    @map("schedule_id") @db.Uuid
  boardingStandId     String    @map("boarding_stand_id") @db.Uuid
  droppingStandId     String    @map("dropping_stand_id") @db.Uuid
  totalFareAmount     BigInt    @map("total_fare_amount") @db.BigInt
  totalFareCurrency   String    @default("INR") @map("total_fare_currency") @db.VarChar(3)
  fareBreakdownJson   Json      @default("{\"_v\":1}") @map("fare_breakdown_json") @db.JsonB
  status              String    @default("PENDING") @db.VarChar(30)
  confirmedAt         DateTime? @map("confirmed_at") @db.Timestamptz
  checkedInAt         DateTime? @map("checked_in_at") @db.Timestamptz
  cancelledAt         DateTime? @map("cancelled_at") @db.Timestamptz
  completedAt         DateTime? @map("completed_at") @db.Timestamptz
  expiredAt           DateTime? @map("expired_at") @db.Timestamptz
  isTest              Boolean   @default(false) @map("is_test")
  createdAt           DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz
  updatedAt           DateTime  @default(now()) @map("updated_at") @db.Timestamptz
  deletedAt           DateTime? @map("deleted_at") @db.Timestamptz
  version             Int       @default(0)

  user           User               @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  schedule       Schedule           @relation(fields: [scheduleId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  boardingStand  Stand              @relation("BoardingStand", fields: [boardingStandId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  droppingStand  Stand              @relation("DroppingStand", fields: [droppingStandId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  passengers     BookingPassenger[]
  ticket         Ticket?
  payments       Payment[]
  gstInvoice     GstInvoice?

  @@index([userId, status, createdAt])
  @@index([scheduleId])
  @@map("bookings")
}

model Payment {
  id                 String    @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid

  gatewayOrderId     String?   @map("gateway_order_id") @db.VarChar(100)

  succeededAt        DateTime? @map("succeeded_at") @db.Timestamptz

  deletedAt          DateTime? @map("deleted_at") @db.Timestamptz

  booking        Booking           @relation(fields: [bookingId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  refunds        Refund[]
  webhooks       GatewayWebhook[]

  @@index([bookingId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([gatewayPaymentId])
  @@map("payments")
}

model Trip {
  id           String    @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  scheduleId   String    @map("schedule_id") @db.Uuid
  busId        String    @map("bus_id") @db.Uuid
  driverId     String    @map("driver_id") @db.Uuid
  status       String    @default("SCHEDULED") @db.VarChar(20)
  startedAt    DateTime? @map("started_at") @db.Timestamptz
  endedAt      DateTime? @map("ended_at") @db.Timestamptz
  cancelledAt  DateTime? @map("cancelled_at") @db.Timestamptz
  delayReason  String?   @map("delay_reason") @db.VarChar(50)
  delayMinutes Int?      @map("delay_minutes")
  distanceKm   Decimal?  @map("distance_km") @db.Decimal(8, 2)
  isTest       Boolean   @default(false) @map("is_test")
  createdAt    DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamptz
  updatedAt    DateTime  @default(now()) @map("updated_at") @db.Timestamptz
  deletedAt    DateTime? @map("deleted_at") @db.Timestamptz
  version      Int       @default(0)

  schedule Schedule       @relation(fields: [scheduleId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  bus      Bus            @relation(fields: [busId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  driver   DriverProfile  @relation(fields: [driverId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  gps      GpsSample[]
  scans    BoardingScan[]

  @@index([scheduleId])
  @@index([driverId, status])
  @@map("trips")
}

model GpsSample {
  id          String   @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  tripId      String   @map("trip_id") @db.Uuid
  lat         Decimal  @db.Decimal(9, 6)
  lng         Decimal  @db.Decimal(9, 6)
  speedKmh    Decimal? @map("speed_kmh") @db.Decimal(5, 2)
  accuracyM   Decimal? @map("accuracy_m") @db.Decimal(5, 2)
  clientReportedSpeedKmh Decimal? @map("client_reported_speed_kmh") @db.Decimal(5, 2)
  receivedAt  DateTime @map("received_at") @db.Timestamptz
  recordedAt  DateTime @map("recorded_at") @db.Timestamptz

  trip Trip @relation(fields: [tripId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([tripId, recordedAt])
  @@map("gps_samples")
}

model BoardingScan {
  id         String    @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  tripId     String    @map("trip_id") @db.Uuid
  ticketId   String    @map("ticket_id") @db.Uuid
  driverId   String    @map("driver_id") @db.Uuid
  standId    String    @map("stand_id") @db.Uuid
  result     String    @db.VarChar(30)
  scannedAt  DateTime  @default(now()) @map("scanned_at") @db.Timestamptz
  scannedLat Decimal?  @map("scanned_lat") @db.Decimal(9, 6)
  scannedLng Decimal?  @map("scanned_lng") @db.Decimal(9, 6)
  isTest     Boolean   @default(false) @map("is_test")

  trip   Trip   @relation(fields: [tripId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  ticket Ticket @relation(fields: [ticketId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  driver DriverProfile @relation(fields: [driverId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  stand  Stand  @relation(fields: [standId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  @@unique([ticketId, tripId], map: "uniq_boarding_scans_ticket_trip")
  @@index([tripId])
  @@index([ticketId])
  @@index([scannedAt])
  @@map("boarding_scans")
}

model AuditLog {
  id           String   @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  actorId      String?  @map("actor_id") @db.Uuid
  sessionId    String?  @map("session_id") @db.Uuid
  entityType   String   @map("entity_type") @db.VarChar(50)
  entityId     String   @map("entity_id") @db.Uuid
  action       String   @db.VarChar(50)
  reason       String?  @db.VarChar(500)
  ipAddress    String?  @map("ip_address") @db.VarChar(45)
  metadataJson Json     @default("{\"_v\":1}") @map("metadata_json") @db.JsonB
  occurredAt   DateTime @default(now()) @map("occurred_at") @db.Timestamptz

  @@index([actorId, occurredAt])
  @@index([entityType, entityId, occurredAt])
  @@index([action, occurredAt])
  @@map("audit_logs")
}
